yam dhyātvā munayaḥ prapañca rahitam vindanti mokṣam dhruvam yam dhyāyanti budhāḥ samādhi samaye śuddham viyat saṃnibham tam saṃsāra vināśa hetum ajaram vande harim mukti dam aśvatthaiḥ pārijātaiḥ ca candana aguru pāṭalaiḥ anyaiḥ ca bahubhiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ campaka ādyaiḥ ca śobhite yava godhūma caṇakaiḥ māṣa mudga tila ikṣubhiḥ cīnaka ādyaiḥ tathā medhyaiḥ sasyaiḥ ca anyaiḥ ca śobhite tān āgatān dvijān te tu pūjām cakruḥ yathocitām tatra ājagāma sūtaḥ tu matimān lomaharṣaṇaḥ tam dṛṣṭvā te muni varāḥ pūjām cakruḥ mudā anvitāḥ kathām cakruḥ tadā anyonyam sūtena sahitāḥ dvijāḥ kathā ante vyāsa śiṣyam te papracchuḥ saṃśayam mudā ṛtvigbhiḥ sahitāḥ sarve sadasyaiḥ saha dīkṣitāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ purāṇa āgama śāstrāṇi sa itihāsāni sattama jānāsi deva daityānām caritam janma karma ca purāṇe mokṣa śāstre ca sarvajñaḥ asi mahāmate śrotum icchāmahe sūta brūhi sarvam yathā jagat lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ aṅguṣṭhāt brahmaṇaḥ jajñe dakṣaḥ kila śubha vrataḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca nāradaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca cākṣuṣasya antare pūrve ṛcaḥ brahmarṣi satkṛtāḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca śṛṇudhvam kīrtayiṣyāmi pṛthoḥ vaiṇyasya vistaram veṇaḥ uvāca pṛthuḥ uvāca bahūn vā prāṇinaḥ anantam bhavet tasya iha pātakam lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca ṛṣayaḥ ca tadā devāḥ pitaraḥ atha sarīsṛpāḥ daityāḥ yakṣāḥ puṇyajanāḥ gandharvāḥ parvatāḥ nagāḥ ete purā dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ duduhuḥ dharaṇīm kila kṣīram vatsaḥ ca pātram ca teṣām dogdhā pṛthak pṛthak ṛṣīṇām abhavat somaḥ vatsaḥ dogdhā bṛhaspatiḥ kṣīram teṣām tapaḥ brahma pātram chandāṃsi bho dvijāḥ pitṝṇām rājatam pātram yamaḥ vatsaḥ pratāpavān antakaḥ ca abhavat dogdhā kṣīram teṣām sudhā smṛtā nāgānām takṣakaḥ vatsaḥ pātram ca alābu saṃjñakam dogdhā tu airāvataḥ nāgaḥ teṣām kṣīram viṣam smṛtam asurāṇām madhuḥ dogdhā kṣīram māyā mayam smṛtam yakṣāṇām āma pātram tu vatsaḥ vaiśravaṇaḥ prabhuḥ sumālī rākṣasa indrāṇām vatsaḥ kṣīram ca śoṇitam dogdhā ca suruciḥ kṣīram teṣām gandhaḥ śuciḥ smṛtaḥ śailam pātram parvatānām kṣīram ratna oṣadhīḥ tathā plakṣaḥ vatsaḥ tu vṛkṣāṇām dogdhā śālaḥ tu puṣpitaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca prajāpatiḥ ca tapasā saṃhāram teṣu nityaśas lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca saṃjñā uvāca savarṇā uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca vivasvān uvāca mūrdhajeṣu nijagrāha sa tu tām muni sattamāḥ tvaṣṭā uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca mitrāvaruṇau ūcatuḥ tatra eva raivataḥ jñātvā yathātattvam dvijottamāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca uttaṅkaḥ uvāca bṛhadaśvaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca vasiṣṭhaḥ uvāca divam āropayām āsa sa śarīram mahā tapāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca tatra ekā jagṛhe putrān ṣaṣṭi sāhasra saṃmitān lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca rudraḥ ca pārṣṇim jagrāha gṛhītvā ājagavam dhanuḥ iṣīkāstambam āsādya garbham sā ca utsasarja ha tadā tam mūrdhni ca āghrāya somaḥ rājā sutam prati lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca ṛcīkaḥ uvāca bhṛgoḥ jagatyām vaṃśe asmin jamadagniḥ ajāyata lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca deva asurāḥ ūcuḥ dānavāḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca anenasaḥ samākhyātāḥ kṣatravṛddhasya ca aparaḥ vairasya antaḥ mahābhāgāḥ kṛtaḥ ca ātmīya tejasā iti ete kāśyapāḥ proktāḥ nahuṣasya nibodhata lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca yayātiḥ uvāca yatra gāthāḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ gītāḥ kila yayātinā yadoḥ tu vaṃśam vakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvam rāja satkṛtam brāhmaṇāḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca śṛṇudhvam muni śārdūlāḥ puroḥ vaṃśam mahātmanaḥ sudhanvanaḥ subāhuḥ ca raudrāśvaḥ tasya ca ātmajaḥ upadānavī tatas putrān caturaḥ ajanayat śubān jahnuḥ api abravīt gaṅgām kruddhaḥ viprāḥ tadā nṛpaḥ bāhlikasya tu rājarṣeḥ vaṃśam śṛṇuta bho dvijāḥ nāradaḥ uvāca calat ūrmi sahasreṇa śaṅkitā abhyeti narmadā prāvartayat tadā rājā sahasreṇa ca bāhunā śrutvā tu baddham paulastyam rāvaṇam tu arjunena ca kroṣṭoḥ vaṃśam muni śreṣṭhāḥ śṛṇudhvam gadataḥ mama lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca yāḥ patnyaḥ vasudevasya caturdaśa vara aṅganāḥ jijñāsām pauruṣe cakre na caskande ca pauruṣam vṛtta pūrva ardha kāyaḥ tu siṃha saṃhananaḥ yuvā lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca rājā uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca dhātrī uvāca gāndinīm nāma tasyāḥ ca gāḥ sadā pradadau pitā lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca balarāmaḥ uvāca śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ sṛṣṭiḥ prajāpateḥ samyak tvayā proktā mahāmate lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca iti ete hi mayā proktāḥ maryādāparvatāḥ dvijāḥ sarvasya ādhāra bhūtaḥ asau dvijāḥ āste akhila ātmakaḥ na eteṣu bhaumāni anyāni kṣudh pipāsā ādi naḥ dvijāḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca ṛkṣa pāda udbhavāḥ hi etāḥ śrutāḥ pāpam haranti yāḥ bhavanti bhūyas puruṣāḥ manuṣyāḥ saṃnyasya viṣṇau paramātma rūpe avāpya tām karma mahīm anante tasmin layam ye tu amalāḥ prayānti jānīma no tat kūvayam vilīne prāpsyanti dhanyāḥ khalu te manuṣyāḥ nava varṣam ca bho viprāḥ jambūdvīpam idam mayā lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca tretā yuga samaḥ kālaḥ sarvadā eva dvijottamāḥ varṇāḥ ca tatra catvāraḥ tān budhāḥ pravadāmi vaḥ plakṣaḥ tad nāma saṃjñaḥ ayam plakṣadvīpaḥ dvijottamāḥ śālmanaḥ ca samudraḥ asau dvīpena ikṣu rasa udakaḥ varṇāḥ ca śālmale ye ca vasanti eṣu dvijottamāḥ tasyām vasanti manujaiḥ saha daiteya dānavāḥ varṣa acalāḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ teṣām nāmāni bho dvijāḥ puṣkalā puṣkarāḥ dhanyāḥ te khyātāḥ ca dvijottamāḥ tatra nadyaḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ yāḥ pibanti tu te sadā tathā raivatakaḥ śyāmaḥ tathā eva ambhogiriḥ dvijāḥ nivasanti mahātmānaḥ nirātaṅkāḥ nirāmayāḥ ādhāra bhūtā jagatām sarveṣām sā dvijottamāḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca nāgānām ca mahāṅgānām jñātayaḥ ca dvijottamāḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca tatas ca anantaram viprāḥ narakāḥ raurava ādayaḥ bhrūṇa hā pura hantā ca go ghnaḥ ca muni sattamāḥ surā paḥ brahma hā hartā suvarṇasya ca śūkare sa yāti kṛmibhakṣye vai kṛmīśe tu duriṣṭi kṛt lālābhakṣye sa yāti ugre śara kartā ca vedhake prayānti ete viśasane narake bhṛśa dāruṇe kṛmipūye naraḥ ca ekaḥ yāti miṣṭānna bhuj sadā vikretā brāhmaṇaḥ yāti tam eva narakam dvijāḥ yānti tatra eva te viprāḥ yaḥ ca apākeṣu vahni daḥ vidyā avidye hi bho viprāḥ jñānam eva avadhāryatām munayaḥ ūcuḥ kathitam bhavatā sarvam asmākam sakalam tathā samācakṣva mahābhāga yathāvat lomaharṣaṇa lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca sanandana ādyāḥ kathitāḥ viprāḥ ca amala cetasaḥ sa bhūrlokaḥ samākhyātaḥ vistāraḥ asya mayā uditaḥ ete sapta mahā lokāḥ mayā vaḥ kathitāḥ dvijāḥ etat aṇḍa kaṭāhena tiryak ūrdhvam adhas tathā sa ca ambu parivāraḥ asau vahninā veṣṭitaḥ bahis ākāśaḥ api muni śreṣṭhāḥ mahatā pariveṣṭitaḥ tathā eva apariṇāmena viśvasya bhagavān hariḥ kartā kriyāṇām sa ca ijyate kratuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca yena viprāḥ vidhānena tat me śṛṇuta sāṃpratam tasya saṃsparśa nirdhūta pāpa paṅkaḥ dvijottamāḥ tat sūrya raśmibhiḥ sadyas samādāya nirasyate ādhāra bhūtaḥ savituḥ dhruvaḥ muni vara uttamāḥ evam mayā muni śreṣṭhāḥ brahmāṇḍam samudāhṛtam munayaḥ ūcuḥ lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca manaḥ viśuddham puruṣasya tīrtham vācām tathā ca indriya nigrahaḥ ca etāni tīrthāni śarīra jāni saṃkṣepeṇa muni śreṣṭhāḥ pṛthivyām yāni kāni vai puṇyam ca magadha araṇyam daṇḍaka araṇyam eva ca gayā prabhāsam śrītīrtham divyam kanakhalam tathā bhṛgutuṅgam hiraṇyākṣam bhīmāraṇyam kuśasthalīm loha ākulam sa kedāram mandara araṇyam eva ca mahābalam koṭitīrtham sarva pāpa haram tathā rūpatīrtham śūkaravam cakratīrtham mahā phalam tīrtham kokāmukham puṇyam badarīśailam eva ca somatīrtham tuṅgakūṭam tīrtham skandāśramam tathā koṭitīrtham ca agnipadam tīrtham pañcaśikham tathā dharmodbhavam koṭitīrtham tīrtham bādhapramocanam gaṅgādvāram pañcakūṭam madhyakesaram eva ca cakraprabham mataṅgam ca kruśadaṇḍam ca viśrutam daṃṣṭrākuṇḍam viṣṇutīrtham sārvakāmikam eva ca brahmakuṇḍam vahnikuṇḍam tīrtham satyapadam tathā catur srotaḥ catur śṛṅgam śailam dvādaśa dhārakam mānasam sthūlaśṛṅgam ca sthūla daṇḍam tathā urvaśī sadāprabham merukuṇḍam tīrtham soma abhiṣecanam mahāsrotam koṭarakam pañcadhāram tridhārakam bilvaprabham devahradam tīrtham viṣṇuhradam tathā śaṅkhaprabham devakuṇḍam tīrtham vajrāyudham tathā vidyādharam sa gāndharvam śrītīrtham brahmaṇaḥ hradam sātīrtham lokapāla ākhyam maṇipura girim tathā tīrtham pañcahradam ca eva puṇyam piṇḍārakam tathā malavyam goprabhāvam ca govaram vaṭamūlakam snāna daṇḍam prayāgam ca guhyam viṣṇupadam tathā kanyāśramam vāyukuṇḍam jambūmārgam tathā uttamam gabhastitīrtham ca tathā yayātipatanam śuci koṭitīrtham bhadravaṭam mahākālavanam tathā piṅgutīrtham sa vāsiṣṭham tīrtham ca pṛthasaṃgamam tīrtham daurvāsikam nāma tathā piñjarakam śubham ṛṣitīrtham brahmatuṅgam vasutīrtham kumārikam śakratīrtham pañcanadam reṇukātīrtham eva ca maṇimattam ca kāma ākhyam kṛṣṇatīrtham kuśāvilam puṣpanyāsam puṇḍarīkam maṇipūram tathā uttaram dīrghasattram hayapadam tīrtham ca anaśanam tathā vastrā padam dāruvalam chāyā ārohaṇam eva ca siddheśvaram mitravalam kālikāśramam eva ca vaṭāvaṭam bhadravaṭam kauśāmbī ca divākaram dvīpam sārasvatam ca eva vijayam kāma dam tathā rudrakoṭim sumanasam tīrtham sa drāva nāmitam satatam pṛthivī sarvam pāriplava pṛthūdakau koṭitīrtham pañcanadam vārāham yakṣiṇīhradam puṇḍarīkam somatīrtham muñjavaṭam tathā uttamam lokadvāram pañcatīrtham kapilātīrtham eva ca sūryatīrtham śaṅkhinī ca gavām bhavanam eva ca tīrtham ca yakṣarājasya brahmāvartam sutīrthakam kāmeśvaram mātritīrtham tīrtham śītavanam tathā snāna loma apaham ca eva māsa saṃsarakam tathā daśāśvamedham kedāram brahmodumbaram eva ca saptarṣi kuṇḍam ca tathā tīrtham devyāḥ sujambukam īṭa āspadam koṭikūṭam kiṃdānam kiṃjapam tathā kāraṇḍavam ca avedhyam ca triviṣṭapam atha aparam pāṇiṣātam miśrakam ca madhūvaṭa manojavau kauśikī devatīrtham ca tīrtham ca ṛṇamocanam divyam ca nṛgadhūma ākhyam tīrtham viṣṇupadam tathā amarāṇām hradam puṇyam koṭitīrtham tathā aparam śrīkuñjam śāli tīrtham ca naimiṣeyam ca viśrutam brahmatīrtham manastīrtham tīrtham vai kārupāvanam īśānatīrtham pravaram pāvanam pāñcayajñikam triśūla dhāram māhendram devasthānam kṛta ālayam śākaṃbharī deva tīrtham suvarṇa ākhyam kilam hradam kṣīraśravam virūpākṣam bhṛgutīrtham kuśodbhavam kubjāmbakam bhadravaṭam vasiṣṭhapadam eva ca rudrāvartam sugandhāśvam kapilā vanam eva ca bhadrakarṇa hradam ca eva śaṅkukarṇa hradam tathā kapālamocanam ca eva avakīrṇam ca kāmyakam sthāṇutīrtham kuroḥ tīrtham svargadvāram kuśadhvajam viśveśvaram mānavakam kūpam nārāyaṇa āśrayam gaṅgāhradam vaṭam ca eva badarīpāṭanam tathā indramārgam eka rātram kṣīraka āvāsam eva ca somatīrtham dadhīcam ca śrutatīrtham ca bho dvijāḥ koṭitīrtha sthalīm ca eva bhadrakālī hradam tathā aśvavedī kubjāvanam yamunāprabhavam tathā vīram pramokṣam sindhūttham ṛṣa kulyā sakṛttikam urvī saṃkramaṇam ca eva māyā vidyā udbhavam tathā mahā āśramaḥ vaitasika rūpam sundarikāśramam bāhutīrtham cārunadīm vimalāśokam eva ca somatīrtham sitodam ca tīrtham matsyodarīm tathā aruṇāspadam kāma dam ca śukratīrtham sa vālukam kuṇḍam vimaladaṇḍasya tīrtham caṇḍeśvarasya ca jyeṣṭhasthāna hradam ca eva puṇyam brahmasaram tathā jaigīṣavya guhā ca eva harikeśavanam tathā ajāmukha saram ca eva ghaṇṭākarṇa hradam tathā puṇḍarīka hradam ca eva vāpī karkoṭakasya ca suvarṇasya udapānam ca śvetatīrtha hradam tathā kuṇḍam ghargharikāyāḥ ca śyāma kūpam ca candrikā śmaśāna stambha kūpam ca vināyaka hradam tathā kūpam sindhūdbhavam ca eva puṇyam brahmasaram tathā rudrāvāsam tathā tīrtham nāgatīrtham pulomakam bhaktahradam kṣīrasaraḥ pretādhāram kumārakam divyam brahmasaram puṇyam gayāśīrṣa akṣayam vaṭam kapilāhradam gṛdhravaṭam sāvitrīhradam eva ca prabhāsanam sītavanam yonidvāram ca dhenukam pitṛkūpam rudratīrtham śakratīrtham sumālinam brahmasthānam sapta kuṇḍam maṇi ratna hradam tathā kauśikyam bharatam ca eva tīrtham jyeṣṭhālikā tathā viśveśvaram kalpa saraḥ kanyāsaṃvetyam eva ca niścīvā prabhavaḥ ca eva vasiṣṭha āśramam eva ca devakūṭam ca kūpam ca vasiṣṭha āśramam eva ca vīrāśramam brahmasaraḥ brahmavīrāvakāpilī kumāradhārā śrīdhārā gaurīśikharam eva ca kumāra vāsam śrī vāsam aurvī śīta artham eva ca saṃdhyātīrtham kāratoyam kapilam lohitārṇavam śoṇodbhavam vaṃśagulmam ṛṣabham kalatīrthakam puṇyāvatīhradam tīrtham tīrtham badarikāśramam rāmatīrtham pitṛvanam virajātīrtham eva ca mārkaṇḍeya vanam ca eva kṛṣṇatīrtham tathā vaṭam sānugartam sa māhendram śrītīrtham śrīnadam tathā iṣutīrtham vārṣabham ca kāverīhradam eva ca kanyātīrtham ca gokarṇam gāyatrīsthānam eva ca jātīhradam devakūpam kuśapravaṇam eva ca tathā anyat vālakhilyānām sa pūrvāṇām tathā aparam tathā anyat ca mahā ṛṣīṇām akhaṇḍita hradam tathā devān ṛṣīn manuṣyān ca pitṝn saṃtarpya ca kramāt pṛthak pṛthak phalam teṣu prati tīrtheṣu bho dvijāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ pṛthivyām uttamām bhūmim dharma kāma artha mokṣa dām lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca kurukṣetre samāsīnam vyāsam matimatām varam vasiṣṭhaḥ jaiminiḥ dhaumyaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ atha vālmikiḥ viśvāmitraḥ śatānandaḥ vātsyaḥ gārgyaḥ atha āsuriḥ maudgalyaḥ tṛṇayajñaḥ ca pippalādaḥ akṛtavraṇaḥ śāṇḍilyaḥ ca vibhāṇḍaḥ ca durvāsāḥ lomaśaḥ tathā bhāskariḥ pūraṇaḥ sūtaḥ pulastyaḥ kapilaḥ tathā ulūkaḥ pulahaḥ vāyuḥ devasthānaḥ caturbhujaḥ sanatkumāraḥ pailaḥ ca kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇānubhautikaḥ papracchuḥ saṃśayam sarve tapovana nivāsinaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ kaṣṭe asmin duḥkha bahule niḥsāre bhava sāgare indriya āvarta kalile dṛṣṭa ūrmi śata saṃkule pṛcchāmaḥ tvām mahābhāgam brūhi naḥ muni sattama śreyaḥ kim atra saṃsāre bhairave lomaharṣaṇe pṛthivyām karma bhūmim ca śrotum icchāmahe vayam mokṣakṣetre tathā mokṣam prāpnoti puruṣaḥ sudhīḥ tasmāt brūhi mahā prājña yat pṛṣṭaḥ asi dvijottama vyāsaḥ uvāca śṛṇudhvam munayaḥ sarve vakṣyāmi yadi pṛcchatha yaḥ saṃvādaḥ abhavat pūrvam ṛṣīṇām brahmaṇā saha meru pṛṣṭhe tu vistīrṇe nānā ratna vibhūṣite nānā varṇa śilā ākīrṇe nānā dhātu vibhūṣite nānā muni jana ākīrṇe nānā āśrama samanvite nānā kusuma gandha āḍhye dakṣiṇa anila sevite bhṛgu ādyāḥ tam tadā devam praṇipatya pitāmaham imam artham ṛṣi varāḥ papracchuḥ pitaram dvijāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ bhagavan śrotum icchāmaḥ karma bhūmim mahī tale vyāsaḥ uvāca purāṇam veda saṃbaddham bhukti mukti pradam śubham pṛthivyām bhāratam varṣam karma bhūmiḥ udāhṛtā karmaṇaḥ phala bhūmiḥ ca svargam ca narakam tathā tasmin varṣe naraḥ pāpam kṛtvā dharmam ca bho dvijāḥ brāhmaṇa ādyāḥ svakam karma kṛtvā samyak su saṃyatāḥ prāpnuvanti parām siddhim tasmin varṣe na saṃśayaḥ prāpnoti puruṣaḥ sarvam tasmin varṣe su saṃyataḥ indra ādyāḥ ca surāḥ sarve tasmin varṣe dvijottamāḥ anye api lebhire mokṣam puruṣāḥ saṃyata indriyāḥ ye ca api svarge tiṣṭhanti vimānena gata jvarāḥ te api kṛtvā śubham karma tasmin varṣe divam gatāḥ nivāsam bhārate varṣe ākāṅkṣanti sadā surāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ tasmāt varṣam idam nātha ye vā asmin varṣaparvatāḥ bhedāḥ ca tasya varṣasya brūhi sarvān aśeṣatas śṛṇudhvam bhāratam varṣam nava bhedena bho dvijāḥ taiḥ pīyante sarit śreṣṭhāḥ tāḥ budhyadhvam dvijottamāḥ māṭharāḥ ca eva kanakāḥ kaikeyāḥ dambhamālikāḥ lampakāḥ śunaśokāḥ ca kulikāḥ jāṅgalaiḥ saha andhāḥ vāmaṅkurākāḥ ca vallakāḥ ca makhāntakāḥ bhadratuṅgāḥ pratijayāḥ bhāryāṅgāḥ cāpamardakāḥ ṛṣikāḥ muṣikāḥ ca eva kumārāḥ rāmaṭhāḥ śakāḥ malajāḥ karkaśāḥ ca eva melakāḥ colakaiḥ saha abhayāḥ ruṇḍikerāḥ ca carcarāḥ hotradhartayaḥ tat etat bhāratam varṣam sarva bījam dvijottamāḥ prayānti karma bhūḥ viprāḥ na anyā lokeṣu vidyate na bhārata samam varṣam pṛthivyām asti bho dvijāḥ yatra vipra ādayaḥ varṇāḥ prāpnuvanti abhivāñchitam tīrtha yātrā phalam ca eva guru sevā phalam tathā devatā ārādhana phalam svādhyāyasya phalam dvijāḥ yatra devāḥ sadā hṛṣṭāḥ janma vāñchanti śobhanam nānā vrata phalam ca eva nānā śāstra phalam tathā ahiṃsā ādi phalam samyak phalam sarva abhivāñchitam iṣṭāpūrta phalam ca eva tathā anyat śubha karmaṇām kaḥ śaknoti guṇān vaktum bhāratasya akhilān dvijāḥ tatra āste bhārate varṣe dakṣiṇa udadhi saṃsthitaḥ samudrāt uttaram tāvat yāvat viraja maṇḍalam deśaḥ asau puṇya śīlānām guṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ alaṃkṛtaḥ tatra deśa prasūtāḥ ye brāhmaṇāḥ saṃyata indriyāḥ śrāddhe dāne vivāhe ca yajñe vā ācārya karmaṇi praśastāḥ sarva kāryeṣu tatra deśa udbhavāḥ dvijāḥ itihāsa vidaḥ ca eva purāṇa artha viśāradāḥ sarva śāstra artha kuśalāḥ yajvānaḥ vīta matsarāḥ putra dāra dhanaiḥ yuktāḥ dātāraḥ satya vādinaḥ itare api trayaḥ varṇāḥ kṣatriya ādyāḥ su saṃyatāḥ sva karma niratāḥ śāntāḥ tatra tiṣṭhanti dhārmikāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ śrotum icchāma tat brūhi kṣetram sūryasya sāṃpratam tasmin deśe suraśreṣṭha yatra āste sa divākaraḥ sarvatra vālukā ākīrṇe deśe sarva guṇa anvite campaka aśoka bakulaiḥ karavīraiḥ sa pāṭalaiḥ mālatī kunda puṣpaiḥ ca tathā anyaiḥ mallikā ādibhiḥ aśvatthaiḥ saptaparṇaiḥ ca āmraiḥ āmrātakaiḥ tathā āste tatra svayam devaḥ sahasrāṃśuḥ divākaraḥ kṛta śaucaḥ viśuddha ātmā smaran devam divākaram sāgare vidhivat snātvā śarvarī ante samāhitaḥ devān ṛṣīn manuṣyān ca pitṝn saṃtarpya ca dvijāḥ upaviśya udaye kāle prāc mukhaḥ savituḥ tadā vilikhya padmam medhāvī raktacandana vāriṇā aṣṭa pattram kesara āḍhyam vartulam ca ūrdhva karṇikam tāmra abhāve arka pattrasya puṭe kṛtvā tila ādikam pidhāya tat muni śreṣṭhāḥ pātram pātreṇa vinyaset karanyāsa aṅga vinyāsam kṛtvā aṅgaiḥ hṛdaya ādibhiḥ piṅga akṣam dvi bhujam raktam padma pattra aruṇa ambaram udyantam bhāskaram dṛṣṭvā sāndra sindūra saṃnibham kṛtvā śirasi tat pātram ekacittaḥ tu vāgyataḥ tri akṣareṇa tu mantreṇa sūryāya arghyam nivedayet agni nirṛti vāyu īśa madhya pūrva ādi dikṣu ca japtvā stutvā namaḥ kṛtvā mudrām baddhvā visarjayet ye vā arghyam saṃprayacchanti sūryāya niyata indriyāḥ tāvat na pūjayet viṣṇum śaṃkaram vā sureśvaram ādityāya śuciḥ bhūtvā puṣpaiḥ gandhaiḥ manoramaiḥ evam dadāti yaḥ ca arghyam saptamyām su samāhitaḥ ādityāya śuciḥ snātaḥ sa labhet īpsitam phalam vidyām prāpnoti vidyā arthī suta arthī putravān bhavet yam yam kāmam abhidhyāyan sūryāya arghyam prayacchati snātvā vai sāgare dattvā sūryāya arghyam praṇamya ca tatas sūryālayam gacchet puṣpam ādāya vāgyataḥ praviśya pūjayet bhānum kṛtvā tu tris pradakṣiṇam evam saṃpūjya tam devam sahasrāṃśum jagatpatim sapta avarān sapta parān vaṃśān uddhṛtya bho dvijāḥ puṇya kṣayāt iha āyātaḥ pravare yoginām kule śayana utthāpane bhānoḥ saṃkrāntyām viṣuva ayane ye tatra yātrām kurvanti śraddhayā saṃyata indriyāḥ ye tam paśyanti kāmārim snātvā samyak mahā udadhau rājasūya phalam samyak vājimedha phalam tathā śāṃkaram yogam āsthāya tatas mokṣam vrajanti te yaḥ tatra savituḥ kṣetre prāṇān tyajati mānavaḥ evam mayā muni śreṣṭhāḥ proktam kṣetram su durlabham munayaḥ ūcuḥ śrutaḥ asmābhiḥ suraśreṣṭha bhavatā yat udāhṛtam tava vaktra udbhavām puṇyām ādityasya agha nāśinīm atas param sura śreṣṭha brūhi naḥ vadatām vara arghaḥ ca kīdṛśaḥ proktaḥ kutra vā saṃpradīyate arghyam pūjā ādikam sarvam bhāskarasya dvijottamāḥ bhaktim śraddhām samādhim ca kathyamānam nibodhata manasā bhāvanā bhaktiḥ iṣṭā śraddhā ca kīrtyate dhyānam samādhiḥ iti uktam śṛṇudhvam su samāhitāḥ tad kathām śrāvayet yaḥ tu tad bhaktān pūjayīta vā agni śuśrūṣakaḥ ca eva sa vai bhaktaḥ sanātanaḥ tad cittaḥ tad manāḥ ca eva deva pūjā rataḥ sadā deva arthe kriyamāṇāni yaḥ karmāṇi anumanyate kīrtanāt vā paraḥ viprāḥ sa vai bhaktataraḥ naraḥ bhāva śuddhiḥ prayoktavyā niyama ācāra saṃyutā arka saṃpuṭa saṃyuktam udakam prasṛtam pibet krama vṛddhyā caturviṃśam ekaikam kṣapayet punar dvābhyām saṃvatsarābhyām tu samāpta niyamaḥ bhavet sarva kāma pradā hi eṣā praśastā hi arkasaptamī ādityam pūjayet yaḥ tu cakṣuṣā na sa hīyate dīpa dātā naraḥ nityam jñāna dīpena dīpyate tilāḥ pavitram tailam vā tila godānam uttamam dātā dīpyati ca api evam na tiryaggatim āpnuyāt jvalamānam sadā dīpam na haret na api nāśayet dīpa hartā naraḥ bandham nāśam krodham tamaḥ vrajet yaḥ samālabhate nityam kuṅkuma aguru candanaiḥ saṃpadyate naraḥ pretya dhanena yaśasā śriyā pratimāyām tathā piṇḍyām deyam arghyam prayatnataḥ na apasavyam na savyam ca dadyāt abhimukhaḥ sadā śrīvāsam caturasram ca devadārum tathā eva ca karpūra agaru dhūpāni dattvā vai svarga gāminaḥ kṛsaraiḥ pāyasaiḥ pūpaiḥ phala mūla ghṛta odanaiḥ kṣīreṇa tarpaṇam kṛtvā manaḥ tāpaiḥ na yujyate dadhnā tu tarpaṇam kṛtvā kārya siddhim labhet naraḥ snāna artham āharet yaḥ tu jalam bhānoḥ samāhitaḥ śraddhayā bhānave dattvā gatim iṣṭām avāpnuyāt munayaḥ ūcuḥ bhūyas prabrūhi deveśa yat pṛcchāmaḥ jagatpate kutas hi asya akṣayaḥ svargaḥ kutas niḥśreyasam param na atas parataraḥ devaḥ kaścid anyaḥ dvijottamāḥ eṣa bhāti tapati eṣa varṣati eṣa gabhastibhiḥ pitṝṇām ca pitā hi eṣa devatānām hi devatā dhruvam sthānam smṛtam hi etat yasmāt na cyavate punar vānaprasthāḥ ca ye ca anye vyāsa ādyāḥ bhikṣavaḥ tathā sthitā vanaspatau sā tu oṣadhīṣu ca sarvatas mūrtiḥ ṣaṣṭhī raveḥ yā tu aryamā iti viśrutā vāyoḥ saṃsaraṇā sā tu deveṣu eva samāśritā bhūyiṣu avasthitā sā tu śarīreṣu ca dehinām vāyau pratiṣṭhitā sā tu prahlādayati vai prajāḥ evam sā jagatām mūrtiḥ hitā vihitā purā tasmāt dhyeyaḥ namasyaḥ ca dvādaśa sthāsu mūrtiṣu iti evam dvādaśa ādityān namaskṛtvā tu mānavaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ yadi tāvat ayam sūryaḥ ca ādidevaḥ sanātanaḥ tatas kasmāt tapaḥ tepe vara īpsuḥ prākṛtaḥ yathā etat vaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi param guhyam vibhāvasoḥ prāk mayā uktāḥ tu yuṣmabhyam raveḥ dvādaśa mūrtayaḥ mitraḥ ca varuṇaḥ ca ubhau tāsām tapasi saṃsthitau abbhakṣaḥ varuṇaḥ tāsām tasthau paścima sāgare nāradaḥ tu mahā yogī sarvān lokān caran vaśī tam dṛṣṭvā tu tapasyantam tasya kautūhalam hi abhūt yaḥ pitā sarva devānām parāṇām api yaḥ paraḥ ayajat devatāḥ kāḥ tu pitṝn vā kān asau yajet iti saṃcintya manasā tam devam nāradaḥ abravīt nāradaḥ uvāca catvāraḥ ca āśramāḥ deva gṛhastha ādyāḥ tathā eva hi kṣetrāṇi iha śarīrāṇi teṣām ca eva yathāsukham saṃjñām tathā mune saḥ ayam brahma ādiṣu tathā āpnuyāt yadā sa budhyati ātmānam tadā bhavati kevalaḥ tam devāḥ sva āśrama sthāḥ ca nānā mūrti samāśritāḥ evam matvā yathājñānam pūjayāmi divākaram etat api adhikam teṣām yat ekam praviśanti uta evam etat purā ākhyātam nāradāya tu bhānunā mayā api ca samākhyātā kathā bhānoḥ dvijottamāḥ idam ākhyānam ākhyeyam mayā ākhyātam dvijottamāḥ jijñāsuḥ labhate jñānam gatim iṣṭām tathā eva ca kṣaṇena labhate adhvānam idam yaḥ paṭhate mune jagat prabhāvāt viśate bhāskarāt vāritaskarāt ṛtu svabhāva varṇaiḥ ca sūryaḥ kṣema subhikṣa kṛt vivasvān iva parjanyaḥ varuṇaḥ ca aryamā tathā mitra vat bhagavān tvaṣṭā sahasreṇa śatena ca sahasreṇa tu mitraḥ vai pūṣā tu navabhiḥ śataiḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ teṣām bhavati kim puṇyam gatiḥ ca parameśvara śṛṇudhvam muni śārdūlāḥ sāra bhūtam sanātanam tāni vaḥ kīrtayiṣyāmi śṛṇudhvam bhāskarasya vai ekaviṃśati iti eṣa stavaḥ iṣṭaḥ sadā raveḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ nirguṇaḥ śāśvataḥ devaḥ tvayā proktaḥ divākaraḥ punar dvādaśadhā jātaḥ śrutaḥ asmābhiḥ tvayā uditaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ bhāskaraḥ jātaḥ tatra naḥ saṃśayaḥ mahān dakṣasya hi sutāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ babhūvuḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ śobhanāḥ vinatā ādyāḥ tathā ca anyāḥ suṣuvuḥ sthānu jaṅgamān bhūmau ca saṃsthitam bhāsvat jvālābhiḥ ati durdṛśam tam dṛṣṭvā ca tatas devī sādhvasam paramam gatā adṛśyata tadā ādityaḥ tapta tāmra upamaḥ prabhuḥ praṇatām aditim viprāḥ prasāda su mukhaḥ vibhuḥ sūryaḥ uvāca kaśyapaḥ uvāca vāc uvāca munayaḥ ūcuḥ katham kāntataram paścāt rūpam saṃlabdhavān raviḥ dvau putrau su mahābhāgau kanyām ca yamunām tathā saṃjñā uvāca chāyā uvāca chāyā uvāca nirguṇeṣu api apatyeṣu mātā śāpam na dāsyati sā tat pariharantī ca śāpāt bhītā tadā raveḥ jagat ākulam atyartham tadā āsīt muni sattamāḥ trailokyam ākulam vīkṣya bhramamāṇam sura ṛṣayaḥ agniḥ ādyāḥ ca bhāsvantam likhyamānam mudā yutāḥ mūrchanābhiḥ ca tālaiḥ ca saṃprayogaiḥ sukha pradam evam janma raveḥ pūrvam babhūva muni sattamāḥ rūpam ca paramam tasya mayā saṃparikīrtitam munayaḥ ūcuḥ tatas vai su mahā tejāḥ prādurbhūtaḥ tamonudaḥ bhūtādiḥ bhūḥ bhuvaḥ svar ca mahaḥ satyam tapaḥ janaḥ pradīptam dīpanam divyam sarva loka prakāśakam namaḥ namaḥ kāraṇa kāraṇāya devāḥ ūcuḥ tava ati taijasam rūpam na kaścid soḍhum utsahet śakra ādyaiḥ saṃstutam devam ye namasyanti bhāskaram munayaḥ ūcuḥ nāmnām aṣṭaśatam brūhi yat tvayā uktam purā raveḥ bhāskarasya param guhyam svarga mokṣa pradam dvijāḥ sraṣṭā saṃvartakaḥ vahniḥ sarvasya ādiḥ a lolupaḥ jayaḥ viśālaḥ vara daḥ sarva bhūta niṣevitaḥ manaḥ suparṇaḥ bhūtādiḥ śīghragaḥ prāṇa dhāraṇaḥ nāmnām aṣṭaśatam ramyam mayā proktam dvijottamāḥ sūrya udaye yaḥ su samāhitaḥ paṭhet smṛtim ca medhām ca sa vindate parām prakīrtayet śuddha manāḥ samāhitaḥ vidrāvya vibudhān sarvān siddha vidyādharān ṛṣīn gandharva yakṣa nāgān ca tathā anyān ca samāgatān jaghāna pūrvam dakṣasya yajataḥ dharaṇī tale śāntim na lebhire viprāḥ kailāsam śaraṇam gatāḥ mahādevaḥ akale deśe kṛttivāsāḥ vṛṣadhvajaḥ ekāmrake muni śreṣṭhāḥ sarva kāma pradaḥ haraḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ jaghāna yajñam dakṣasya devaiḥ sarvaiḥ alaṃkṛtam dakṣasya āsan aṣṭa kanyāḥ yāḥ ca evam pati saṃgatāḥ dakṣaḥ uvāca śrī śaṃkaraḥ uvāca dakṣaḥ uvāca kṛtvā āhutim tava krūra apaḥ spṛśanti karmasu munayaḥ ūcuḥ katham roṣeṇa sā pūrvam dakṣasya duhitā satī tyaktvā deham punar jātāḥ giri rāja gṛhe prabho deha antare katham tasyāḥ pūrva dehaḥ babhūva ha bhavena saha saṃyogaḥ saṃvādaḥ ca tayoḥ katham vivāhaḥ ca jagannātha sarva āścarya samanvitaḥ śrotum icchāmahe puṇyām kathām ca ati manoharām kaśyapaḥ uvāca mama akhyātiḥ apatyena brahmaṇā ṛṣibhiḥ saha athā agāt tatra śaila indra vipraḥ niyamavān śuciḥ pitaraḥ ūcuḥ kaśyapaḥ uvāca tasmāt kṛtvā tapaḥ ghoram apatyam guṇavat bhṛśam tapaḥ cakāra api atulam yena tuṣṭiḥ abhūt mama tadā tam utpapāta aham vara daḥ asmi iti ca abravam himavān uvāca āsīt tasya ekaparṇā tu devalam suṣuve sutam vayam sadā eva yasya ime vaśyāḥ vai kiṃkarāḥ śubhe udāra rūpaḥ vikṛta ādi rūpaḥ vibhīṣaṇam rūpam iva āsthitaḥ yaḥ devi śīghreṇa kālena dhūrjaṭiḥ nīlalohitaḥ sā devī sūktam iti evam uktvā svasya āśrame śubhe tam uvāca argha pādyābhyām madhuparkeṇa ca eva ha śivaḥ uvāca śivaḥ uvāca yaḥ hi yāsyati puṇya arthī saḥ aśvamedham avāpsyati sarasi udaka saṃpūrṇe samīpe ca āśramasya ca bālaḥ uvāca aho bata su kaṣṭam vai yaḥ aham bālaḥ a kṛta āśramaḥ pārvatī uvāca grāhaḥ uvāca grāhaḥ uvāca yat bravīmi kuru śreṣṭhe tathā mokṣam avāpsyati grāhaḥ uvāca grāhaḥ uvāca dehena api mayā grāha rakṣyaḥ vipraḥ prayatnataḥ tapaḥ punar mayā prāpyam na prāpyaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ punar bāla anubhāva ācaraṇam hi śaṃbhoḥ deva ādayaḥ sarva jagat nivāsāḥ samāyayuḥ tatra gṛhīta veśāḥ praphulla padmāsana saṃniviṣṭaḥ vijñāpitaḥ tena mahīdhra rājñā akṣṇām sahasram surarāj sa bibhrat divya aṅga hāra sraj udāra rūpaḥ āruhya sarva amara rāj sa vajram bibhrat samāgāt puratas surāṇām tejaḥ prabhāva adhika tulya rūpī ārūḍhaḥ āgāt tvaritam javena maṇi pradīpta ujjvala kuṇḍalaḥ ca āditya madhyāt bhaga nāmadhārī pīna aṅgayaṣṭiḥ su kṛta aṅgahāra tejaḥ bala ājñā sadṛśa prabhāvaḥ daṇḍam samāgṛhya kṛtāntaḥ āgāt āruhya bhīmam mahiṣam javena mahā mahīdhra ucchraya pīna gātraḥ svarṇa ādi ratna āñcita cāru veśaḥ saṃtāpayan sarva sura asura īśān nānā maṇi prajvalita aṅgayaṣṭiḥ āruhya sarva draviṇa adhipa īśaḥ āpyāyayan sarva sura asura īśān kāntyā ca veśena ca cāru rūpaḥ śyāma aṅgayaṣṭiḥ su vicitra veśaḥ sarva aṅgaḥ ābaddha sugandhi mālyaḥ tārkṣyam samāruhya mahīdhra kalpam atha aśvinau ca api bhiṣagvarau dvau bibhrat tadānīm jvalana arka tejāḥ sārdham sa nāgaiḥ aparaiḥ mahātmā diteḥ sutānām ca mahā asurāṇām vara anurūpam pravidhāya veśam vṛndam samāgāt puratas surāṇām gandharva rājaḥ sa ca cāru rūpī śakra ājñayā tatra samājagāma anye ca devāḥ tridivāt tadānīm pṛthak pṛthak cāru gṛhīta veśāḥ ājagmuḥ āruhya vimāna pṛṣṭham rarāja rāja adhika lakṣya mūrtiḥ ājñā bala aiśvarya kṛta pramodaḥ svayaṃvaram tam samalaṃcakāra mātā ca teṣām sa sura asurāṇām patnī ca śaṃbhoḥ puruṣasya dhīmataḥ gītā purāṇe prakṛtiḥ parā yā dakṣasya kopāt himavat gṛham sā kārya artham āyāt tridivaukasām hi vimāna pṛṣṭhe maṇi hema juṣṭe sthitā valat cāmara vījita aṅgī sarva ṛtu puṣpām su sugandha mālām nivṛttā ca tadā tasthau kṛtvā sā hṛdi tam vibhum vavande caraṇam śaṃbhoḥ stutavān tam aham dvijāḥ himavān uvāca saurabheyam manaḥ ramyam gandham ādāya mārutaḥ pratyagra saṃjāta śilīndhra kandalī latā druma ādi udgata pallavā śubhā śubha ambu dhārā praṇaya prabodhitaiḥ mahā alasaiḥ bheka gaṇaiḥ ca nāditā priyeṣu māna uddhata mānasānām manasvinīnām api kāminīnām mayūra kekā abhirutaiḥ kṣaṇena tathā vivarṇa ujjvala cāru mūrtinā śaśāṅka lekhā kuṭilena sarvatas mahā indracāpena bhṛśam virājitā sura aṅganānām alaka āvalīḥ śubhāḥ nirīkṣitā sa ādaram utsukābhiḥ niśvāsa dhūmram pathika aṅganābhiḥ surabhi kusuma reṇu kḥpta sarva aṅga śobhā giriduhitṛ vivāhe prāvṛṣ āvirbabhūva nānā puṣparajaḥ sugandhi pavana prahrādanī cetasām tatra āsīt kalahaṃsa nūpura ravā devyāḥ vivāhe śarad ṛtu pāryaya saṃprāptaḥ babhūva sa mahā giriḥ manobhava udreka karāḥ surāṇām surāṅganānām ca muhur samīrāḥ svaccha ambu pūrṇāḥ ca tathā nalinyaḥ padma utpalānām kusumaiḥ upetāḥ vivāhe guru kanyāyāḥ vasantaḥ samagāt ṛtuḥ nāryaḥ yathā ramyatarāḥ babhūvuḥ na ati uṣṇa śītāni payaḥ sarāṃsi kiñjalka cūrṇaiḥ kapilīkṛtāni yayuḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ sura danti mukhyāḥ tālāḥ stamālāḥ saralāḥ kapitthāḥ aśoka sarja arjuna kovidārāḥ puṃnāga nāgeśvara karṇikārāḥ lavaṅga tāla aguru saptaparṇā nyagrodha śobhāñjana nārikelāḥ vṛkṣāḥ tathā anye phala puṣpavantaḥ dṛśyāḥ babhūvuḥ su manohara aṅgāḥ jalāśayāḥ ca eva suvarṇa toyāḥ cakrāṅga kāraṇḍava haṃsa juṣ koyaṣṭi dātyūha balāka yuktā dṛśyāḥ tu padma utpala mīna pūrṇāḥ khagāḥ ca nānāvidha bhūṣita aṅgāḥ dṛśyāḥ tu vṛkṣeṣu su citra pakṣāḥ krīḍāsu yuktān atha tarjayantaḥ kurvanti śabdam madana īrita aṅgāḥ vavuḥ ca vātāḥ sukha śītala aṅgāḥ puṣpāṇi śubhrāṇi api pātayantaḥ śanais nagebhyaḥ malaya adri jātāḥ tathā eva sarve ṛtavaḥ ca puṇyāḥ cakāśire anyonya vimiśrita aṅgāḥ yeṣām su liṅgāni ca kīrtitāni te tatra āsan su manojña rūpāḥ nīlāni nīla amburuhaiḥ payāṃsi gaurāṇi gauraiḥ ca mṛṇāla daṇḍaiḥ raktaiḥ ca raktāni bhṛśam kṛtāni haimāni vistīrṇa jaleṣu kaścitsu nirantaram cārutarāṇi kaścitsu vaidūrya nālāni saraḥsu kaścitsu nikāma puṣpaiḥ su viśāla śākhaiḥ himācalaḥ atīva tadā rarāja cañcat pakṣāḥ sa madhurataram nīlakaṇṭhāḥ vineduḥ teṣām śabdaiḥ upacita balaḥ puṣpacāpa iṣu hastaḥ sajjībhūtaḥ tridaśa vanitāḥ veddhum aṅgeṣu anaṅgaḥ vividha vihaṃgama nāda ramya deśāḥ ṣaṭ upayayuḥ ṛtavaḥ muni pravīrāḥ nānā vādya samākīrṇe aham tatra dvijātayaḥ mām āha śaṃkaraḥ ca evam devadevaḥ jagatpatiḥ śivaḥ uvāca tatas ca aham prahṛṣṭa ātmā kuśān ādāya sa tvaram devāḥ ūcuḥ suvarṇaretase ca eva namaḥ kuṇḍala dhāriṇe kāla jñāya ca sarveṣām namaḥ niyama kāriṇe śrī śaṃkaraḥ uvāca varam varayata kṣipram dātāsmi tam asaṃśayam devāḥ ūcuḥ saḥ apratirūpa gaṇa īśa samānaḥ vipra varyāḥ stavam hi imam śṛṇuyāt vā paṭhet ca yaḥ umā maheśvarau ūcatuḥ dagdhvā kāmam tatas viprāḥ sa tu devaḥ vṛṣadhvajaḥ reme tatra umayā sārdham prahṛṣṭaḥ tu himācale nadī tīreṣu kānteṣu kiṃnara ācariteṣu ca śṛṅgeṣu śailarājasya taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca vana rājiṣu ramyāsu nānā pakṣi ruteṣu ca eteṣu puṇyeṣu manohareṣu deśeṣu vidyādhara bhūṣiteṣu tasmin nage harṣam avāpa śaṃbhuḥ nṛtyanti tatra apsarasaḥ sura īśāḥ gāyanti gandharva gaṇāḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ mahā balaiḥ śakra yama agni tulyaiḥ devyāḥ priya artham bhaga netra hantā girim na tatyāja tadā mahātmā ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ devyāḥ samam tu bhagavān tiṣṭhan tatra sa kāma hā devīm bāla indu tilakaḥ ramayan ca rarāma ca āsanena mahārheṇa śaṃpādayat aninditām pārvatī uvāca śrutvā sa devyāḥ vacanam sureśaḥ tasyāḥ priya arthe sva girim vihāya bhāryā sahāyaḥ sva gaṇaiḥ ca yuktaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ katham vināśitaḥ yajñaḥ dakṣasya amita tejasaḥ mahādevena roṣāt vai tat naḥ prabrūhi vistarāt varṇayiṣyāmi vaḥ viprāḥ mahādevena vai yathā krodhāt vidhvaṃsitaḥ yajñaḥ devyāḥ priya cikīrṣayā svarga sthānāt atha āgamya dakṣam āpedire tathā aśvinau marutaḥ ca eva nānā deva gaṇaiḥ saha āgatāḥ sattriṇaḥ sarve devāḥ strībhiḥ saha ṛṣibhiḥ dadhīciḥ uvāca dadhīciḥ uvāca dakṣaḥ uvāca dadhīciḥ uvāca dakṣaḥ uvāca dakṣaḥ uvāca viṣṇoḥ ca bhāgāḥ vividhāḥ pradattāḥ dadāmi bhāgam na tu śaṃkarāya yajñam etam mahābhāga kimartham na anugacchasi pūrva āgatena gantavyam mārgeṇa varavarṇini kim nāma dānam niyamam tapaḥ vā evam bruvāṇām bhagavān vicintya patnīm prahṛṣṭaḥ kṣubhitām uvāca na vetsi mām devi kṛśa udara aṅgi aham vijānāmi viśāla netre mām adhvara īśam nitarām stuvanti mām brāhmaṇāḥ brahma mantraiḥ yajanti uvāca saḥ asṛjat bhagavān vaktrāt bhūtam krodha agni saṃbhavam nāśaya āśu kratum tasya dakṣasya mad anujñayā rudrasya anucarāḥ sarve sarve rudra parākramāḥ samabhūt su mahān viprāḥ sarva rudra gaṇaiḥ kṛtaḥ rudra kopāḥ mahā kopāḥ kālāgni sadṛśa upamāḥ bhakṣayantaḥ atha śaila ābhāḥ bhīṣayantaḥ ca sarvatas vīrabhadra iti vikhyātaḥ rudra kopāt viniḥsṛtaḥ tadā ca aham mahādevam abravam pratipūjayan dakṣaḥ api manasā devam bhavam śaraṇam anvagāt śrāvite ca mahā ākhyāne devānām pitṛbhiḥ saha dakṣaḥ uvāca evam dṛṣṭvā tadā dakṣaḥ śaṃbhoḥ vīryam dvijottamāḥ dakṣaḥ uvāca deva indra tvam bala śreṣṭha deva dānava pūjita mūrtimān tvam mahā mūrtiḥ samudraḥ sarasām nidhiḥ śatru ghātāya caṇḍāya parṇa saṃgha śayāya ca bādhā apahāya lubdhāya gīta vāditra kāriṇe pavanāya pataṃgāya namaḥ sāṃkhya parāya ca hāyi hāyi hare hāyi huvāhāva iti vā asakṛt yajuḥ mayaḥ ṛk mayaḥ ca sāma atharva yutaḥ tathā prāṇaḥ ca tvam rajaḥ ca tvam tamaḥ sattva yutaḥ tathā matsyaḥ jālaḥ jalaḥ ajayyaḥ jalavyālaḥ kuṭīcaraḥ ghaṇṭākī ghaṇṭakī ghaṇṭī cūḍālaḥ lavaṇa udadhiḥ brahmāvartaḥ surāvartaḥ kāmāvarta namaḥ astu te daṇḍadhṛk cakradaṇḍaḥ ca raudrabhāgavināśanaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca uvāca dakṣa duḥkham na kartavyam yajña vidhvaṃsanam prati aham yajña hanaḥ tubhyam dṛṣṭam etat purā anagha imām jvara utpattim adīna mānasaḥ paṭhet sadā yaḥ su samāhitaḥ naraḥ labheta kāmān ca yathā manīṣitān iha loke sukham prāpya gaṇa rāj upajāyate vṛṣeṇa viniyuktena vimānena virājate lomaharṣaṇaḥ uvāca rudra krodha udbhavām puṇyām vyāsasya vadataḥ dvijāḥ pārvatyāḥ ca tathā roṣam krodham śaṃbhoḥ ca duḥsaham dakṣa yajña vināśam ca vīryam śaṃbhoḥ tathā adbhutam punar prasādam devasya dakṣasya su mahātmanaḥ papracchuḥ ca punar vyāsam kathā śeṣam tathā dvijāḥ pṛṣṭaḥ provāca tān vyāsaḥ kṣetram ekāmrakam punar vyāsaḥ uvāca brahma proktām kathām puṇyām śrutvā tu ṛṣi puṃgavāḥ praśaśaṃsuḥ tadā hṛṣṭāḥ romāñcita tanūruhāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ aho devasya māhātmyam tvayā śaṃbhoḥ prakīrtitam dakṣasya ca suraśreṣṭha yajña vidhvaṃsanam tathā vyāsaḥ uvāca provāca śaṃbhoḥ tat kṣetram bhū tale duṣkṛta chadam liṅga koṭi samāyuktam vārāṇasī samam śubham ekāmraka iti vikhyātam tīrtha aṣṭaka samanvitam ekāmra vṛkṣaḥ tatra āsīt purā kalpe dvijottamāḥ bhūyiṣṭham dhana dhānya ādi saṃyutam gṛha gopura saṃbādham tri kacā dvāra bhūṣitam rājahaṃsa nibhaiḥ śubhraiḥ prāsādaiḥ upaśobhitam mārga ga dvāra saṃyuktam sita prākāra śobhitam rakṣitam śastra saṃghaiḥ ca parikhābhiḥ alaṃkṛtam sita raktaiḥ tathā pītaiḥ kṛṣṇa śyāmaiḥ ca varṇakaiḥ vīṇā veṇu mṛdaṅgaiḥ ca kṣepaṇībhiḥ alaṃkṛtam devatāyatanaiḥ divyaiḥ prākāra udyāna maṇḍitaiḥ sthira alakāḥ su kapolāḥ kāñcī nūpura nāditāḥ su keśyaḥ cāru jaghanāḥ karṇa anta āyata locanāḥ divya vastra dharāḥ śubhrāḥ kāścid kāñcana saṃnibhāḥ haṃsa vāraṇa gāminyaḥ kuca bhāra avanāmitāḥ mada alasāḥ ca su śroṇyaḥ nityam prahasita ānanāḥ īṣat vispaṣṭa daśanāḥ bimba oṣṭhāḥ madhura svarāḥ tāmbūla rañjita mukhāḥ vidagdhāḥ priya darśanāḥ divya vastra dharāḥ sarvāḥ sadā cāritra maṇḍitāḥ sve sve gṛhe pramuditāḥ divā rātrau vara ānanāḥ anyāḥ ca tatra tiṣṭhanti vāramukhyāḥ su locanāḥ urvaśī sadṛśāḥ ca eva vipracitti nibhāḥ tathā sarvāḥ tāḥ priya vādinyaḥ sarvāḥ vihasita ānanāḥ kalā kauśala saṃyuktāḥ sarvāḥ tāḥ guṇa saṃyutāḥ evam paṇya striyaḥ tatra nṛtya gīta viśāradāḥ nivasanti muni śreṣṭhāḥ sarva strī guṇa garvitāḥ prekṣaṇa ālāpa kuśalāḥ sundaryaḥ priya darśanāḥ na rūpa hīnāḥ durvṛttāḥ na para droha kārikāḥ yāsām kaṭākṣa pātena moham gacchanti mānavāḥ na rūpa hīnāḥ durvṛttāḥ na para droha kāriṇaḥ tiṣṭhanti mānavāḥ tatra kṣetre jagati viśrute cūta nimba kadambaiḥ ca tathā anyaiḥ puṣpa jātibhiḥ palāśaiḥ mucukundaiḥ ca pārijātaiḥ sa kubjakaiḥ kadalī vana khaṇḍaiḥ ca jambū pūga phalaiḥ tathā ketakī karavīraiḥ ca atimuktaiḥ ca kiṃśukaiḥ mandāra kunda puṣpaiḥ ca tathā anyaiḥ puṣpa jātibhiḥ nānā pakṣi gaṇaiḥ ca anyaiḥ kūjadbhiḥ madhura svaraiḥ dīrghikābhiḥ taḍāgaiḥ ca puṣkariṇībhiḥ ca vāpibhiḥ evam nānāvidhaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ puṣpaiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ varaiḥ nānā jalāśayaiḥ puṇyaiḥ śobhitam tat samantataḥ āste tatra svayam devaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ vṛṣadhvajaḥ tebhyaḥ pūrvam samāhṛtya jala bindūn pṛthak pṛthak cakāra ṛṣibhiḥ sārdham tena bindusaraḥ smṛtam aṣṭamyām bahule pakṣe mārgaśīrṣe dvijottamāḥ devān ṛṣīn manuṣyān ca pitṝn saṃtarpya vāgyataḥ graha uparāge viṣuve saṃkrāntyām ayane tathā yuga ādiṣu ṣaḍaśītyām tathā anyatra śubhe tithau piṇḍam ye saṃprayacchanti pitṛbhyaḥ sarasaḥ taṭe tatas śaṃbhoḥ gṛham gatvā vāgyataḥ saṃyata indriyaḥ praviśya pūjayet śarvam kṛtvā tam tris pradakṣiṇam ghṛta kṣīra ādibhiḥ snānam kārayitvā bhavam śuciḥ a dīkṣitaḥ tu nāmnā eva mūlamantreṇa ca arcayet evam saṃpūjya tam devam gandha puṣpa anurāgibhiḥ nṛtya japya namaskāraiḥ jaya śabdaiḥ pradakṣiṇaiḥ uddyotayan diśaḥ sarvāḥ śiva lokam sa gacchati tatas tasmāt iha āyātaḥ pṛthivyām puṇya saṃkṣaye jāyate yoginām gehe catur vedī dvijottamāḥ śayana utthāpane ca eva saṃkrāntyām ayane tathā aśokākhyām tathā aṣṭamyām pavitra āropaṇe tathā tasmin kṣetra vare liṅgam bhāskareśvara saṃjñitam paśyanti ye tu tam devam snātvā kuṇḍe maheśvaram ādityena arcitam pūrvam devadevam trilocanam tiṣṭhanti tatra muditāḥ kalpam ekam dvijottamāḥ puṇya kṣayāt iha āyātāḥ jāyante pravare kule mokṣa śāstra artha kuśalāḥ sarvatra sama buddhayaḥ yogam śaṃbhoḥ varam prāpya tatas mokṣam vrajanti te tasmin kṣetra vare puṇye liṅgam yat dṛśyate dvijāḥ pūjya apūjyam ca sarvatra vane rathyā antare api vā catuṣpathe śmaśāne vā yatra kutra ca tiṣṭhati dṛṣṭvā tat liṅgam avyagraḥ śraddhayā su samāhitaḥ daṇḍa vat praṇipātaiḥ ca nṛtya gīta ādibhiḥ tathā saṃpūjya evam vidhānena śiva lokam vrajet naraḥ nārī vā dvija śārdūlāḥ saṃpūjya śraddhayā anvitā kaḥ śaknoti guṇān vaktum samagrān muni sattamāḥ tasya kṣetra varasya atha ṛte devān maheśvarāt mādhava ādiṣu māseṣu naraḥ vā yadi vā aṅganā paśyet devam virūpākṣam devīm ca vara dām śivām gaṇam caṇḍam kārttikeyam gaṇeśam vṛṣabham tathā kalpadrumam ca sāvitrīm śiva lokam sa gacchati snātvā ca kāpile tīrthe vidhivat pāpa nāśane ekāmrake śiva kṣetre vārāṇasī same śubhe viraje virajā mātā brahmāṇī saṃpratiṣṭhitā yasyāḥ saṃdarśanāt martyaḥ punāti āsaptamam kulam sakṛt dṛṣṭvā tu tām devīm bhaktyā āpūjya praṇamya ca anyāḥ ca tatra tiṣṭhanti viraje loka mātaraḥ āste vaitaraṇī tatra sarva pāpa harā nadī āste svayaṃbhūḥ tatra eva kroḍa rūpī hariḥ svayam dṛṣṭvā praṇamya tam bhaktyā param viṣṇum vrajanti te kāpile gograhe some tīrthe ca alābu saṃjñite mṛtyuṃjaye kroḍatīrthe vāsuke siddhakeśvare kāle tu muni śreṣṭhāḥ veditavyāni tāni vai āste guhyam param kṣetram mukti dam pāpa nāśanam sarvatra vālukā ākīrṇam pavitram sarva kāma dam aśoka arjuna puṃnāgaiḥ bakulaiḥ sarala drumaiḥ panasaiḥ nārikelaiḥ ca śālaiḥ tālaiḥ kapitthakaiḥ sarja bhūrja aśvakarṇaiḥ ca tamālaiḥ devadārubhiḥ kharjūra āmrātakaiḥ siddhaiḥ mucukundaiḥ sa kiṃśukaiḥ aśvatthaiḥ saptaparṇaiḥ ca madhudhāra śubhāñjanaiḥ śiṃśapā āmalakaiḥ nīpaiḥ nimba tindu vibhītakaiḥ manaḥ hlāda karaiḥ śubhraiḥ nānā vihaga nāditaiḥ mālatī kunda bāṇaiḥ ca karavīraiḥ sitetaraiḥ mātuluṅgaiḥ pūga phalaiḥ hintālaiḥ kadalī vanaiḥ anyaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ puṣpaiḥ ca anyaiḥ manoharaiḥ latā vitāna gulmaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ ca jalāśayaiḥ dīrghikābhiḥ taḍāgaiḥ ca puṣkariṇībhiḥ ca vāpibhiḥ dātyūha sārasa ākīrṇaiḥ koyaṣṭi baka śobhitaiḥ aśeṣa vidyā nilayam sarva dharma guṇa ākaram āste tatra muni śreṣṭhāḥ vikhyātaḥ puruṣottamaḥ yāvat utkala maryādā diś krameṇa prakīrtitā tāvat kṛṣṇa prasādena deśaḥ puṇyatamaḥ hi saḥ yatra tiṣṭhati viśvātmā deśe sa puruṣottamaḥ nivasāmaḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ tasmin deśe sadā vayam suparṇāḥ kiṃnarāḥ nāgāḥ tathā anye svarga vāsinaḥ itihāsa purāṇāni yajñāḥ ca vara dakṣiṇāḥ śreṣṭha tvam kasya deśasya kim ca anyat adhikam tatas tīrtha rāja jale snātvā paśyanti puruṣottamam saphalam jīvitam teṣām utkalānām sumedhasām cāru bhrū keśa mukuṭam cāru karṇa avataṃsakam cāru smitam cāru dantam cāru kuṇḍala maṇḍitam su nāsam su kapolam ca su lalāṭam su lakṣaṇam babhūva nṛpatiḥ śrīmān indradyumnaḥ iti śrutaḥ rūpavān subhagaḥ śūraḥ dātā bhoktā priyaṃvadaḥ dhanurvede ca vede ca śāstre ca nipuṇaḥ kṛtī vallabhaḥ nara nārīṇām paurṇamāsyām yathā śaśī ādityaḥ iva duṣprekṣyaḥ śatru saṃgha bhayaṃkaraḥ adhyetā yoga sāṃkhyānām mumukṣuḥ dharma tatparaḥ evam sa pālayan pṛthvīm rājā sarva guṇa ākaraḥ kasmin kṣetre athavā tīrthe nadī tīre tathā āśrame evam cintā paraḥ saḥ atha nirīkṣya manasā mahīm ālokya sarva tīrthāni kṣetrāṇi atha purāṇi api tāni sarvāṇi saṃtyajya jagāma āyatanam punar vikhyātam paramam kṣetram mukti dam puruṣottamam sa gatvā tat kṣetra varam samṛddha bala vāhanaḥ kārayitvā mahā utsedham prāsādam ca eva viśrutam tatra saṃkarṣaṇam kṛṣṇam subhadrām sthāpya vīryavān pañcatīrtham ca vidhivat kṛtvā tatra mahīpatiḥ snānam dānam tapaḥ homam devatā prekṣaṇam tathā mārkaṇḍeyam ca kṛṣṇam ca dṛṣṭvā rāmam ca bho dvijāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ jagāma paramam kṣetram mukti dam puruṣottamam gatvā tatra suraśreṣṭha katham sa nṛpa sattamaḥ katham sa kṛṣṇam rāmam ca subhadrām ca prajāpate nirmame rāja śārdūlaḥ kṣetram rakṣitavān katham katham tatra mahīpālaḥ prāsāde bhuvana uttame sthāpayām āsa matimān kṛṣṇa ādīn tridaśa arcitān vakṣyāmi tasya caritam yathāvṛttam kṛte yuge śṛṇudhvam muni śārdūlāḥ prayatāḥ saṃyata indriyāḥ avantī nāma nagarī mālave bhuvi viśrutā babhūva tasya nṛpateḥ pṛthivī kakuda upamā hṛṣṭa puṣṭa jana ākīrṇā dṛḍha prākāra toraṇā dṛḍha yantra argala dvārā parikhābhiḥ alaṃkṛtā rathyā āpaṇavatī ramyā suvibhakta catuṣpathā gṛha gopura saṃbādhā vīthībhiḥ samalaṃkṛtā rājahaṃsa nibhaiḥ śubhraiḥ citra grīvaiḥ manoharaiḥ aneka śata sāhasraiḥ prāsādaiḥ samalaṃkṛtā samṛddhā sā muni śreṣṭhāḥ vidvadbhiḥ samalaṃkṛtā na tatra malināḥ santi na mūrkhāḥ na api nirdhanāḥ na rogiṇaḥ na hīna aṅgāḥ na dyūta vyasana anvitāḥ sadā hṛṣṭāḥ sumanasaḥ dṛśyante puruṣāḥ striyaḥ krīḍanti sma divā rātrau hṛṣṭāḥ tatra pṛthak pṛthak su veṣāḥ puruṣāḥ tatra dṛśyante mṛṣṭa kuṇḍalāḥ surūpāḥ su guṇāḥ ca eva divya alaṃkāra bhūṣitāḥ su keśāḥ su kapolāḥ ca su mukhāḥ śmaśru dhāriṇaḥ jñātāraḥ sarva śāstrāṇām bhettāraḥ śatru vāhinīm striyaḥ tatra muni śreṣṭhāḥ dṛśyante su manoharāḥ haṃsa vāraṇa gāminyaḥ praphulla ambhoja locanāḥ su keśāḥ cāru vadanāḥ su kapolāḥ sthira alakāḥ hāva bhāva ānata grīvāḥ karṇa ābharaṇa bhūṣitāḥ bimba oṣṭhyaḥ rañjita mukhāḥ tāmbūlena virājitāḥ śyāma avadātāḥ su śroṇyaḥ kāñcī nūpura nāditāḥ vidagdhāḥ subhagāḥ kāntāḥ cāru aṅgyaḥ priya darśanāḥ krīḍantyaḥ ca mada unmattāḥ sabhāsu catvareṣu ca gīta vādya kathā ālāpaiḥ ramayantyaḥ ca tāḥ striyaḥ vāramukhyāḥ ca dṛśyante nṛtya gīta viśāradāḥ prekṣaṇa ālāpa kuśalāḥ sarva yoṣit guṇa anvitāḥ anyāḥ ca tatra dṛśyante guṇa ācāryāḥ kulastriyaḥ pativratāḥ ca subhagāḥ guṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ alaṃkṛtāḥ nāraṅgaiḥ lakucaiḥ lodhraiḥ saptaparṇaiḥ śubhāñjanaiḥ cūta bilva kadambaiḥ ca śiṃśapaiḥ dhava khādiraiḥ pāṭalā aśoka tagaraiḥ karavīraiḥ sitetaraiḥ pīta arjunaka bhallātaiḥ siddhaiḥ āmrātakaiḥ tathā nyagrodha aśvattha kāśmaryaiḥ palāśaiḥ devadārubhiḥ mandāraiḥ pārijātaiḥ ca tintiḍīka vibhītakaiḥ prācīnāmalakaiḥ plakṣaiḥ jambū śirīṣa pādapaiḥ kāleyaiḥ kāñcanāraiḥ ca madhu jambīra tindukaiḥ kharjūra agastya bakulaiḥ śākhoṭaka harītakaiḥ mallikā kunda bāṇaiḥ ca kadalī khaṇḍa maṇḍitaiḥ mātuluṅgaiḥ pūga phalaiḥ karuṇaiḥ sindhuvārakaiḥ anyaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ puṣpa vṛkṣaiḥ ca anyaiḥ manoharaiḥ sadā kusuma gandha āḍhyaiḥ sadā phala bhara ānataiḥ kapotaiḥ khañjarīṭaiḥ ca śyenaiḥ pārāvataiḥ tathā saritaḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ ca sarāṃsi su bahūni ca sārasaiḥ ca balākaiḥ ca kūrmaiḥ matsyaiḥ sa nakrakaiḥ khagaiḥ jalacaraiḥ ca anyaiḥ nānā rava vibhūṣitaiḥ nānā varṇaiḥ sadā hṛṣṭaiḥ añcitāni samantataḥ evam nānāvidhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ vividhaiḥ ca jalāśayaiḥ devatāyatanaiḥ divyaiḥ śobhitā sā mahā purī mahākāla iti vikhyātaḥ sarva kāma pradaḥ śivaḥ śivakuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā vidhivat pāpa nāśane devān pitṝn ṛṣīn ca eva saṃtarpya vidhivat budhaḥ gatvā śivālayam paścāt kṛtvā tam tris pradakṣiṇam praviśya saṃyataḥ bhūtvā dhauta vāsāḥ jita indriyaḥ snānaiḥ puṣpaiḥ tathā gandhaiḥ dhūpaiḥ dīpaiḥ ca bhaktitaḥ naivedyaiḥ upahāraiḥ ca gīta vādyaiḥ pradakṣiṇaiḥ daṇḍa vat praṇipātaiḥ ca nṛtyaiḥ stotraiḥ ca śaṃkaram saṃpūjya vidhivat bhaktyā mahākālam sakṛt śivam puṇya kṣayāt iha āyātaḥ pravare brāhmaṇe kule āste tatra nadī puṇyā śiprā nāma iti viśrutā tam dṛṣṭvā muktim āpnoti tri sapta kula saṃyutaḥ bhuṅkte ca vividhān kāmān nirātaṅkaḥ gata jvaraḥ kālena āgatya matimān brāhmaṇaḥ syāt mahī tale vikramasvāmi nāmānam viṣṇum tatra eva bho dvijāḥ dṛṣṭvā naraḥ vā nārī vā phalam pūrva uditam labhet evam sā nagarī ramyā rāja siṃhena pālitā pura aṣṭādaśa saṃyuktā su vistīrṇa catuṣpathā dhanuḥ jyā ghoṣa ninadā siddha saṃgama bhūṣitā vidyāvat gaṇa bhūyiṣṭhā veda nirghoṣa nāditā kāvya ālāpa kathāḥ ca eva śrūyante aharniśam dvijāḥ evam mayā guṇa āḍhyā sā samudāhṛtā yasyām rājā abhavat pūrvam indradyumnaḥ mahāmatiḥ tasyām sa nṛpatiḥ pūrvam kurvan rājyam anuttamam satya vādī mahā prājñaḥ śūraḥ sarva guṇa ākaraḥ satyavān śīlavān dāntaḥ śrīmān parapuraṃjayaḥ śārada induḥ iva ābhāti lakṣaṇaiḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ āhartā sarva yajñānām hayamedha ādi kṛt tathā suvarṇa maṇi muktānām gaja aśvānām ca bhūpatiḥ hasti aśva ratha mukhyānām kambala ajina vāsasām vicārya sarva śāstrāṇi tantrāṇi āgama vistaram dharma śāstrāṇi sarvāṇi niyamān ṛṣi bhāṣitān ādhāya paramām kāṣṭhām kṛtakṛtyaḥ abhavat tadā bhrānti jñānāt atītaḥ tu mumukṣuḥ saṃyata indriyaḥ katham ārādhayiṣyāmi devadevam sanātanam śrīvatsa uraḥ samāyuktam mukuṭa aṅgada śobhitam sva purāt sa tu niṣkrāntaḥ ujjayinyāḥ prajāpatiḥ anujagmuḥ tu tam sarve rathinaḥ śastra pāṇayaḥ sādinaḥ ca tathā sarve prāsa tomara pāṇayaḥ aśvaiḥ pavana saṃkāśaiḥ anujagmuḥ tu tam nṛpam īṣā dantaiḥ sadā mattaiḥ pracaṇḍaiḥ ṣaṣṭihāyanaiḥ hema kakṣaiḥ sa patākaiḥ ghaṇṭā rava vibhūṣitaiḥ asaṃkhyeyāḥ ca pādātāḥ dhanuṣprāsa asi pāṇayaḥ sarva astra kuśalāḥ śūrāḥ sadā saṃgrāma lālasāḥ su madhyamāḥ cāru veṣāḥ cāru karṇa alaka āñcitāḥ tāmbūla rañjita mukhāḥ rakṣibhiḥ ca su rakṣitāḥ upagīyamānāḥ tāḥ sarvāḥ gāyanaiḥ stuti pāṭhakaiḥ veṣṭitāḥ śastra hastaiḥ ca padma pattra āyata īkṣaṇāḥ vaṇij grāma gaṇāḥ sarve nānā pura nivāsinaḥ dhanaiḥ ratnaiḥ suvarṇaiḥ ca sa dārāḥ sa paricchadāḥ astra vikrayakāḥ ca eva tāmbūla paṇya jīvinaḥ tṛṇa vikrayakāḥ ca eva kāṣṭha vikraya kārakāḥ raṅga upajīvinaḥ sarve māṃsa vikrayiṇaḥ tathā tathā java sahārāḥ ca rajakāḥ ca sahasraśas gopālāḥ nāpitāḥ ca eva tathā anye vastra sūcakāḥ meṣa pālāḥ ca aja pālāḥ mṛga pālāḥ ca haṃsakāḥ guḍa vikrayikāḥ ca eva tathā lavaṇa jīvinaḥ gāyanāḥ nartakāḥ ca eva tathā maṅgalapāṭhakāḥ śailūṣāḥ kathakāḥ ca eva purāṇa artha viśāradāḥ kavayaḥ kāvya kartāraḥ nānā kāvya viśāradāḥ viṣa ghnāḥ gāruḍāḥ ca eva nānā ratna parīkṣakāḥ vyokārāḥ tāmrakārāḥ ca kāṃsyakārāḥ ca rūṭhakāḥ kauṣakārāḥ citrakārāḥ kundakārāḥ ca pāvakāḥ daṇḍakārāḥ ca asikārāḥ surā dhūta upajīvinaḥ mallāḥ dūtāḥ ca kāyasthāḥ ye ca anye karma kāriṇaḥ tantuvāyāḥ rūpakārāḥ vārtikāḥ tailapāṭhakāḥ lāvajīvāḥ taittirikāḥ mṛga pakṣi upajīvinaḥ gaja vaidyāḥ ca vaidyāḥ ca nara vaidyāḥ ca ye narāḥ vṛkṣa vaidyāḥ ca go vaidyāḥ ye ca anye cheda dāhakāḥ yathā vrajantam pitaram grāma antaram samutsukāḥ anuyānti yathā putrāḥ tathā tam te api nāgarāḥ evam sa nṛpatiḥ śrīmān vṛtaḥ sarvaiḥ mahājanaiḥ evam gatvā sa nṛpatiḥ dakṣiṇasya udadheḥ taṭam vīcī taraṅga bahulam mahā āścarya samanvitam tīrtha rājam mahā śabdam apāram su bhayaṃkaram megha vṛnda pratīkāśam agādham makara ālayam matsyaiḥ kūrmaiḥ ca śaṅkhaiḥ ca śuktikā nakra śaṅkubhiḥ śiṃśumāraiḥ karkaṭaiḥ ca vṛtam sarpaiḥ mahāviṣaiḥ lavaṇodam hareḥ sthānam śayanasya nadīpatim aneka āvarta gambhīram dānavānām samāśrayam amṛtasya araṇim divyam devayonim apām patim tīrthānām uttamam tīrtham avyayam yādasām patim upajīvyam ca sarveṣām puṇyam nadanadīpatim vṛtam śālaiḥ kadambaiḥ ca puṃnāgaiḥ sarala drumaiḥ tālaiḥ pippalaiḥ kharjūraiḥ nāraṅgaiḥ bījapūrakaiḥ dāḍimaiḥ ca tamālaiḥ ca pārijātaiḥ tathā arjunaiḥ prācīnāmalakaiḥ bilvaiḥ priyaṅgu vaṭa khādiraiḥ iṅgudī saptaparṇaiḥ ca aśvattha agastya jambukaiḥ palāśa badaraiḥ nīpaiḥ siddhanimba śubhāñjanaiḥ vārakaiḥ kovidāraiḥ ca bhallāta āmalakaiḥ tathā iti hintālaka aṅkolaiḥ karañjaiḥ sa vibhītakaiḥ sa sarja madhu kāśmaryaiḥ śālmalī devadārubhiḥ śākhoṭhakaiḥ nimba vaṭaiḥ kumbhīkā oṣṭha harītakaiḥ guggulaiḥ candanaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ tathā eva aguru pāṭalaiḥ jambīra karuṇaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ tintiḍī raktacandanaiḥ evam nānāvidhaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ tathā anyaiḥ bahu pādapaiḥ mayūra vara saṃghuṣṭaiḥ śuka sārika saṃkulaiḥ hārītaiḥ bhṛṅgarājaiḥ ca cātakaiḥ bahuputrakaiḥ jīvaṃjīvaka kākolaiḥ kalaviṅkaiḥ kapotakaiḥ puṣpita agreṣu vṛkṣeṣu kūjadbhiḥ cāru adhiṣṭhitaiḥ ketakī vana khaṇḍaiḥ ca sadā puṣpa dharaiḥ sitaiḥ kuṭajaiḥ bāṇapuṣpaiḥ ca atimuktaiḥ sa kubjakaiḥ mālatī karavīraiḥ ca tathā kadala kāñcanaiḥ anyaiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ su gandhaiḥ cāru darśanaiḥ muni yakṣa gaṇa ākīrṇaiḥ nānā sattva niṣevitaiḥ tathā anyaiḥ kṛṣṇasāra ādyaiḥ mṛgaiḥ sarvatra śobhitaiḥ śārdūlaiḥ dīpta mātaṅgaiḥ tathā anyaiḥ vana cāribhiḥ latā gulma vitānaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ ca jalāśayaiḥ kādambaiḥ ca plavaiḥ haṃsaiḥ cakravāka upaśobhitaiḥ parvataiḥ dīpta śikharaiḥ cāru kandara maṇḍitaiḥ sarva āścarya mayaiḥ śṛṅgaiḥ sarva bhūta ālayaiḥ śubhaiḥ dadarśa sa mahīpālaḥ sthānam trailokya pūjitam nānā āścarya samāyuktam kṣetram parama durlabham munayaḥ ūcuḥ kim tatra pratimā pūrvam na sthitā vaiṣṇavī prabho sthāpayām āsa kṛṣṇam ca rāmam bhadrām śubha pradām śrotum icchāmahe sarvam brūhi tad kāraṇam ca naḥ pravakṣyāmi samāsena śriyā pṛṣṭaḥ purā hariḥ sumeroḥ kāñcane śṛṅge sarva āścarya samanvite anyaiḥ devālayaiḥ sādhyaiḥ kaśyapa ādyaiḥ prajeśvaraiḥ puṃnāga aśoka sarala nyagrodha āmrātaka arjunaiḥ dhava khādira pālāśa śīrṣa āmalaka tindukaiḥ sarjaiḥ ca karṇaiḥ tagaraiḥ śiśi bhūrja vanimbakaiḥ anyaiḥ ca kāñcanaiḥ ca eva phala bhāraiḥ ca nāmitaiḥ nānā kusuma gandha āḍhyaiḥ bhūṣite puṣpa pādapaiḥ pāṭalā agastya kuṭaja mandāra kusuma ādibhiḥ anyaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ puṣpaiḥ manasaḥ prīti dāyakaiḥ evam nānāvidhaiḥ vṛkṣaiḥ puṣpaiḥ nānāvidhaiḥ tathā śrīḥ uvāca brūhi tvam sarva loka īśa saṃśayam me hṛdi sthitam yena mucyeta deveśa asmāt saṃsāra sāgarāt ācakṣva sarva deva īśa praṇatām yadi manyase tvat ṛte nā asti loke asmin vaktā saṃśaya nirṇaye provāca parayā prītyā param sāra amṛta upamam śrī bhagavān uvāca sukha upāsyaḥ susādhyaḥ ca abhirāmaḥ ca su sat phalaḥ āste tīrtha vare devi vikhyātaḥ puruṣottamaḥ bhāvena ekena suśroṇi śṛṇuṣva varavarṇini tasmin jāgarti bhūtātmā paramātmā jagadguruḥ śrīmān tri mūrti kṛt devaḥ jagatkartā maheśvaraḥ vāsudeva iti vikhyātaḥ yoga ātmā hariḥ īśvaraḥ padma kesara saṃkāśam brahmāṇam bhūtam avyayam tādṛś bhūtaḥ tatas brahmā sarva loka mahā īśvaraḥ pañcabhūta samāyuktam sṛjate ca śanais śanais mātrā yonīni bhūtāni sthūla sūkṣmāṇi yāni ca marīci ādīn munīn sarvān deva asura pitṝn api nara vānara siṃhān ca vividhān ca vihaṃgamān antya jātān ca mlecchān ca sasarja vividhān pṛthak yat kiṃcid jīva saṃjñam tu tṛṇa gulma pipīlikam dakṣiṇa aṅge tathā ātmānam saṃcintya puruṣam svayam vāme ca eva tu nārīm sa dvidhā bhūtam akalpayat adhama uttama madhyāḥ ca mama kṣetrāṇi yāni ca jagāma dhyānam āsthāya vāsudeva ātmikām tanum tuṣṭāva paramaiḥ stotraiḥ viriñciḥ su samāhitaḥ kāraṇam vada mām tāta mama dhyānasya sāṃpratam svarga dvārasya mārgāṇi yajña dāna vratāni ca vihāya sarvam eteṣām sukham tad sādhanam vada sthānam jagatpate mahyām utkṛṣṭam ca yat ucyate sarveṣām uttamam sthānam brūhi me puruṣottama uttamam sarva kṣetrāṇām dhanyam saṃsāra tāraṇam bhukti mukti pradam nṝṇām kṣetram parama durlabham mahā puṇyam tu sarveṣām siddhi dam vai pitāmahe tasmāt āsīt samutpannam tīrtha rājam sanātanam nāga vidyādharāṇām ca sthāvarasya carasya ca uttamaḥ puruṣaḥ yasmāt tasmāt sa puruṣottamaḥ dṛṣṭa mātre vaṭe tasmin chāyām ākramya ca asakṛt pradakṣiṇā kṛtā yaiḥ tu namaskāraḥ ca jantubhiḥ sarve vidhūta pāpmānaḥ te gatāḥ keśava ālayam anāyāsena vai yānti bhuvanam me tatas narāḥ gacchamānān tu tān prekṣya ekadā dharmarāj priye kṣīroda vāsinam devam śeṣa bhoga anuśāyinam varam vareṇyam vara dam kartāram a kṛtam prabhum sarvajñam nirguṇam śāntam jagat dhātāram avyayam purāṇam puruṣam vedyam vyakta avyaktam sanātanam parāvarāṇām sraṣṭāram loka nātham jagadgurum hāra keyūra saṃyuktam mukuṭa aṅgada dhāriṇam kūṭastham acalam sūkṣmam jyotiḥ rūpam sanātanam bhāva abhāva vinirmuktam vyāpinam prakṛteḥ param tam dṛṣṭvā tu mahābhāge praṇatam prāñjali sthitam stotrasya kāraṇam devi pṛṣṭavān aham antakam kimartham stutavān mām tvam saṃkṣepāt tat bravīhi me indranīla mayī śreṣṭhā pratimā sārvakāmikī tām dṛṣṭvā puṇḍarīkākṣa bhāvena ekena śraddhayā śveta ākhyam bhavanam yānti niṣkāmāḥ ca eva mānavāḥ atas kartum na śaknomi vyāpāram ari sūdana yama tām gopayiṣyāmi sikatābhiḥ samantataḥ yathā tatra na paśyanti manujāḥ svarga kāṅkṣiṇaḥ yamam prasthāpayām āsa svām purīm dakṣiṇām diśam luptāyām pratimāyām tu indranīlasya bho dvijāḥ tam sarvam kathayām āsa sa tasyai bhagavān purā lavaṇasya udadheḥ tīre kāṣṭhasya darśanam tathā darśanam vāsudevasya śilpi rājasya ca dvijāḥ nirmāṇam pratimāyāḥ tu yathāvarṇam viśeṣataḥ sthāpanam ca eva sarveṣām prāsāde bhuvana uttame yātrā kāle ca vipra indrāḥ kalpa saṃkīrtanam tathā mārkaṇḍeyasya caritam sthāpanam śaṃkarasya ca pañcatīrthasya māhātmyam darśanam śūlapāṇinaḥ vaṭasya darśanam ca eva vyuṣṭim tasya ca bho dvijāḥ darśanam baladevasya kṛṣṇasya ca viśeṣataḥ subhadrāyāḥ ca tatra eva māhātmyam ca eva sarvaśas darśanam narasiṃhasya vyuṣṭi saṃkīrtanam tathā śvetamādhavamāhātmyam svargadvārasya darśanam pañca tīrtha phalam ca eva mahājyeṣṭham tathā eva ca sthānam kṛṣṇasya halinaḥ parva yātrā phalam tathā varṇanam viṣṇu lokasya kṣetrasya ca punar punar munayaḥ ūcuḥ tasmin kṣetra vare gatvā kim cakāra narādhipaḥ kṣetra saṃdarśanam ca eva kṛtyam tasya ca bhūpateḥ nadī tatra mahā puṇyā vindhya pāda vinirgatā gaṅgā tulyā mahā srotāḥ dakṣiṇa arṇava gāminī mahānadī iti nāmnā sā puṇya toyā sarit varā dakṣiṇasya udadheḥ garbham gatā āvartā ati śobhitā dṛśyante muni śārdūlāḥ su sasyāḥ su manoharāḥ tāmbūla pūrṇa vadanāḥ mālā dāma vibhūṣitāḥ kāvya śāstra kathāḥ tatra śrūyante ca mahājanaiḥ saṃpūrṇa lakṣaṇa upetāḥ vistīrṇa śroṇi maṇḍalāḥ saroruha mukhāḥ śyāmāḥ śarad candra nibha ānanāḥ pīna unnata stanāḥ sarvāḥ samṛddhyā cāru darśanāḥ sauvarṇa valaya ākrāntāḥ divyaiḥ vastraiḥ alaṃkṛtāḥ kadalī garbha saṃkāśāḥ padma kiñjalka saprabhāḥ bimba adhara puṭāḥ kāntāḥ karṇa anta āyata locanāḥ su mukhāḥ cāru keśāḥ ca hāva bhāva avanāmitāḥ kāścid padma palāśa akṣyaḥ kāścid indīvara īkṣaṇāḥ vidyut vispaṣṭa daśanāḥ tanu aṅgyaḥ ca tathā aparāḥ kuṭila alaka saṃyuktāḥ sīmantena virājitāḥ kuṇḍalaiḥ ratna saṃyuktaiḥ karṇapūraiḥ manoharaiḥ deva yoṣit pratīkāśāḥ dṛśyante śubha lakṣaṇāḥ divya gīta varaiḥ dhanyaiḥ krīḍamānāḥ vara aṅganāḥ krīḍantyaḥ tāḥ sadā hṛṣṭāḥ vilāsinyaḥ parasparam evamādi tathā aneka gīta vādya viśāradāḥ divā rātrau samāyuktāḥ kāma unmattāḥ vara aṅganāḥ iti evam dadṛśe rājā kṣetram parama śobhanam jagadgurum param devam param pāram param padam idam tat mānasam tīrtham jñātam me puruṣottamam kalpavṛkṣaḥ mahā kāyaḥ nyagrodhaḥ yatra tiṣṭhati pratimā ca indranīla ākhyā svayam devena gopitā na ca atra dṛśyate ca anyā pratimā vaiṣṇavī śubhā evam sa pṛthivīpālaḥ cintayitvā dvijottamāḥ ānāyya gaṇakān sarvān ācāryān śāstra pāragān bhūmim saṃśodhya yatnena rājā tu parayā mudā amātyaiḥ mantribhiḥ ca eva vāstu vidyā viśāradaiḥ taiḥ sārdham sa samālocya su muhūrte śubhe dine su candra tārā saṃyoge graha ānukūlya saṃyute jaya maṅgala śabdaiḥ ca nānā vādyaiḥ manoharaiḥ puṣpa lāja akṣataiḥ gandhaiḥ pūrṇa kumbhaiḥ sa dīpakaiḥ dadau arghyam tatas rājā śraddhayā su samāhitaḥ kaliṅga adhipatim śūram utkala adhipatim tathā kośala adhipatim ca eva tān uvāca tadā nṛpaḥ rājā uvāca gacchadhvam sahitāḥ sarve śilā arthe su samāhitāḥ gṛhītvā śilpi mukhyān ca śilākarma viśāradān nirūpya sarva sānūni chedayitvā śilāḥ śubhāḥ saṃvāhyantām ca śakaṭaiḥ naukābhiḥ mā vilambatha evam gantum samādiśya tān nṛpān sa mahīpatiḥ rājā uvāca gacchantu dūtāḥ sarvatra mama ājñām pravadantu vai yatra tiṣṭhanti rājānaḥ pṛthivyām tān su śīghra gāḥ evam dūtāḥ samājñātāḥ rājñā tena mahātmanā gatvā tadā nṛpān ūcuḥ vacanam tasya bhūpateḥ śrutvā tu te tathā sarve dūtānām vacanam nṛpāḥ ye nṛpāḥ sarva digbhāge ye ca dakṣiṇatas sthitāḥ paścimāyām sthitāḥ ye ca uttarāpatha saṃsthitāḥ pārvatīyāḥ ca ye kecid tathā dvīpa nivāsinaḥ saṃprāptāḥ bahuśas viprāḥ śrutvā indradyumna śāsanam tān āgatān nṛpān dṛṣṭvā sa amātyān sa purohitān provāca rājā hṛṣṭa ātmā kāryam uddiśya sa ādaram rājā uvāca śṛṇudhvam nṛpa śārdūlāḥ yathā kiṃcid bravīmi aham hayamedham mahā yajñam prāsādam ca eva vaiṣṇavam yadi yūyam sahāyāḥ me bhavadhvam nṛpa sattamāḥ sarve pramuditāḥ hṛṣṭāḥ bhūpāḥ te tasya śāsanāt kambala ajina ratnaiḥ ca rāṅkava āstaraṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ vajra vaidūrya māṇikyaiḥ padmarāga indranīlakaiḥ śāli vrīhi yavaiḥ ca eva māṣa mudga tilaiḥ tathā śyāmākaiḥ madhukaiḥ ca eva nīvāraiḥ sa kulatthakaiḥ anyaiḥ ca vividhaiḥ dhānyaiḥ grāmya āraṇyaiḥ sahasraśas bahu dhānya sahasrāṇām taṇḍulānām ca rāśibhiḥ rājānaḥ pūrayām āsuḥ yat kiṃcid dravya saṃbhavaiḥ tān dṛṣṭvā yajña saṃbhārān sarva saṃpad samanvitān śāstreṣu nipuṇān dakṣān kuśalān sarva karmasu ṛṣīn ca eva mahā ṛṣīn ca deva ṛṣīn ca eva tāpasān snātakān brāhmaṇān ca anyān agnihotre sadā sthitān ācārya upādhyāya varān svādhyāya tapasā anvitān sadasyān śāstra kuśalān tathā anyān pāvakān bahūn dṛṣṭvā tān nṛpatiḥ śrīmān uvāca svam purohitam rājā uvāca kārayām āsa vidhivat śālāḥ tatra api anekaśas bhojanāni vicitrāṇi śāli ikṣu yava gorasaiḥ upetya nṛpati śreṣṭhaḥ vyādideśa svayam tadā tatas śrutvā nṛpa śreṣṭhaḥ kṛtam sarvam atandritaḥ sthala jāḥ jala jāḥ ye ca paśavaḥ kecana dvijāḥ dvijāḥ ca tatra dṛśyante rājñaḥ tasya mahā makhe gṛhītvā bhājanam jagmuḥ bahūni dvijasattamāḥ digdeśāt āgatān rājñaḥ mahā saṃgrāma śālinaḥ naṭa nartakaka ādīn ca gīta stuti viśāradān indīvara palāśa akṣyaḥ śarad candra nibha ānanāḥ kariṇaḥ parvata ākārān mada siktān mahā balān śataśas koṭi saṃghātaiḥ dantibhiḥ danta bhūṣaṇaiḥ vāta vega javaiḥ aśvaiḥ sindhu jātaiḥ su śobhanaiḥ śveta aśvaiḥ śyāmakarṇaiḥ ca koṭi anekaiḥ java anvitaiḥ iti evam dadṛśe rājā yajña saṃbhāra vistaram mudam lebhe tadā rājā saṃhṛṣṭaḥ vākyam abravīt rājā uvāca cārayadhvam pṛthivyām vai rāja putrāḥ su saṃyatāḥ kṛṣṇa chāgam ca mahiṣam kṛṣṇa sāra mṛgam dvijān anaḍvāham ca gāḥ ca eva sarvān ca paśu pālakān iṣṭayaḥ ca pravartantām prāsādam vaiṣṇavam tatas striyaḥ ca ratna koṭyaḥ ca grāmāḥ ca nagarāṇi ca samyak samṛddha bhūmyaḥ ca viṣayāḥ ca evam arthinām anyāni dravya jātāni manojñāni bahūni ca sarveṣām yācamānānām na asti hi etat na bhāṣayet tāvat pravartatām yajñaḥ yāvat devaḥ purā tu iha pratyakṣam mama ca abhyeti yajñasya asya samīpatas evam uktvā tadā viprāḥ rāja siṃhaḥ mahā bhujaḥ dadau suvarṇa saṃghātam koṭīnām ca eva bhūṣaṇam kareṇu śata sāhasram vājinaḥ niyutāni ca arbudam ca eva vṛṣabham svarṇa śṛṅgīḥ ca dhenukāḥ surūpāḥ surabhīḥ ca eva kāṃsya dohāḥ payasvinīḥ su śuklāni ca śubhrāṇi pravāla maṇim uttamam vajra vaidūrya māṇikya muktika ādyāni yāni ca alaṃkāravatīḥ śubhrāḥ kanyāḥ rājīva locanāḥ śatāni pañca viprebhyaḥ rājā hṛṣṭaḥ pradattavān striyaḥ pīna payaḥ bhārāḥ kañcukaiḥ sva stana āvṛtāḥ madhya hīnāḥ ca su śroṇyaḥ padma pattra āyata īkṣaṇāḥ hāva bhāva anvita grīvāḥ bahvyaḥ valaya bhūṣitāḥ pāda nūpura saṃyuktāḥ paṭṭa dukūla vāsasaḥ ekaikaśas adadāt tasmin kāmyāḥ ca kāminīḥ bahūḥ bhakṣyam bhojyam ca saṃpūrṇam nānā saṃbhāra saṃyutam khaṇḍaka ādyāni anekāni svinna pakvān ca piṣṭakān annāni anyāni medhyān ca ghṛta pūrān ca khāṇḍavān madhurān tarjitān pūpān annam mṛṣṭam supākikam prīti artham sarva sattvānām dīyate annam punar punar evam dṛṣṭvā mahā yajñam deva daityāḥ sa vāraṇāḥ purodhāḥ mantriṇaḥ rājā hṛṣṭāḥ tatra eva sarvaśas na tatra malinaḥ kaścid na dīnaḥ na kṣudhā anvitaḥ na akāla maraṇam tatra na daṃśaḥ na grahāḥ viṣam hṛṣṭa puṣṭa janāḥ sarve tasmin rājñaḥ mahā utsave na jātam tādṛśam yajñam dhana dhānya samanvitam evam sa rājā vidhivat vājimedham dvijottamāḥ kratum samāpayām āsa prāsādam vaiṣṇavam tathā munayaḥ ūcuḥ brūhi naḥ devadeveśa yat pṛcchāmaḥ purātanam yathā tāḥ pratimāḥ pūrvam indradyumnena nirmitāḥ kena ca eva prakāreṇa tuṣṭaḥ tasmai sa mādhavaḥ kathayāmi purā vṛttam pratimānām ca saṃbhavam cintā āviṣṭaḥ tu abhūt rājā śete rātrau divā api na śaila mṛd dāru jāteṣu praśastam kim mahī tale etaiḥ eva trayāṇām tu dayitam syāt sura arcitam cintā āviṣṭaḥ mahīpālaḥ saṃstotum upacakrame ratikānta namaḥ te astu trāhi mām saṃvara antaka namaḥ te balinām śreṣṭha namaḥ te lāṅgala āyudha trāhi viṣṇo jagannātha magnam mām bhava sāgare narasiṃha mahā vīrya trāhi mām dīpta locana yathā rasātalāt urvī tvayā daṃṣṭra uddhṛtā purā tathā mahāvarāhaḥ tvam trāhi mām duḥkha sāgarāt tava etāḥ mūrtayaḥ kṛṣṇa vara dāḥ saṃstutāḥ mayā tava ime baladeva ādyāḥ pṛthak rūpeṇa saṃsthitāḥ aṅgāni tava deveśa garutman ādyāḥ tathā prabho dikpālāḥ sa āyudhāḥ ca eva keśava ādyāḥ tathā acyuta ye ca anye tava deveśa bhedāḥ proktāḥ manīṣibhiḥ mayā arcitāḥ stutāḥ sarve tathā yūyam namaskṛtāḥ bhedāḥ te kīrtitāḥ ye tu hare saṃkarṣaṇa ādayaḥ tava pūjā artha saṃbhūtāḥ tatas tvayi samāśritāḥ advaitam tvām katham dvaitam vaktum śaknoti mānavaḥ ekaḥ tvam hi hare vyāpī cit svabhāvaḥ nirañjanaḥ paramam tava yat rūpam bhāva abhāva vivarjitam tat devāḥ ca na jānanti katham jānāmi aham prabho tat arcayanti vibudhāḥ ye ca anye tava saṃśrayāḥ trāhi mām padma pattra akṣa magnam viṣaya sāgare na anyam paśyāmi lokeśa yasya aham śaraṇam vraje tvām ṛte kamalā kānta prasīda madhusūdana jarā vyādhi śataiḥ yuktaḥ nānā duḥkhaiḥ nipīḍitaḥ harṣa śoka anvitaḥ mūḍhaḥ karma pāśaiḥ su yantritaḥ viṣama udaka duṣpāre rāga dveṣa jhaṣa ākule indriya āvarta gambhīre tṛṣṇā śoka ūrmi saṃkule nirāśraye nirālambe niḥsāre atyanta cañcale nānā jāti sahasreṣu jāyamānaḥ punar punar vividhāni anubhūtāni saṃsāre asmin janārdana itihāsa purāṇāni tathā śilpāni anekaśas a saṃtoṣāḥ ca saṃtoṣāḥ saṃcaya apacayāḥ vyayāḥ mayā prāptāḥ jagannātha kṣaya vṛddhi akṣaya itarāḥ bhāryā ari mitra bandhūnām viyogāḥ saṃgamāḥ tathā duḥkhāni ca anubhūtāni yāni saukhyāni anekaśas prāptāḥ ca bāndhavāḥ putrāḥ bhrātaraḥ jñātayaḥ tathā ma yoṣitam tathā strīṇām koṣṭhe viṣ mūtra picchale vārdhake ca hṛṣīkeśa tāni prāptāni vai mayā maraṇe yāni duḥkhāni yama mārge yama ālaye mayā tāni anubhūtāni narake yātanāḥ tathā kṛmi kīṭa drumāṇām ca hasti aśva mṛga pakṣiṇām mahiṣa uṣṭra gavām ca eva tathā anyeṣām vanaukasām dvijātīnām ca sarveṣām śūdrāṇām ca eva yoniṣu dhaninām kṣatriyāṇām ca daridrāṇām tapasvinām nṛpāṇām nṛpa bhṛtyānām tathā anyeṣām ca dehinām gṛheṣu teṣām utpannaḥ deva ca aham punar punar gataḥ asmi dāsa tām nātha bhṛtyānām bahuśas nṛṇām daridra tvam ca īśvara tvam svāmi tvam ca tathā gataḥ hataḥ mayā hatāḥ ca anye ghātitaḥ ghātitāḥ tathā dhaninām śrotriyāṇām ca daridrāṇām tapasvinām uktam dainyam ca vividham tyaktvā lajjām janārdana tathā ca aham suraśreṣṭha karma rajju samāvṛtaḥ bhramāmi su ciram kālam na antam paśyāmi karhicit śoka tṛṣṇā abhibhūtaḥ aham kāṃdiśīkaḥ vicetanaḥ idānīm tvām aham deva vihvalaḥ śaraṇam gataḥ trāhi mām duḥkhitam kṛṣṇa magnam saṃsāra sāgare kṛpām kuru jagannātha bhaktam mām yadi manyase ye tu tvām vidhivat deva na arcayanti nara adhamāḥ sugatiḥ tu katham teṣām bhavet saṃsāra bandhanāt kim teṣām kula śīlena vidyayā jīvitena ca yeṣām na jāyate bhaktiḥ jagaddhātari keśave prakṛtim tu āsurīm prāpya ye tvām nindanti mohitāḥ na teṣām niṣkṛtiḥ tasmāt vidyate naraka arṇavāt ye dūṣayanti durvṛttāḥ tvām deva puruṣa adhamāḥ ārādhya tvām surāḥ daityāḥ narāḥ ca anye api saṃyatāḥ avāpuḥ paramām siddhim kaḥ tvām deva na pūjayet na śaknuvanti brahma ādyāḥ stotum tvām tridaśāḥ hare katham mānuṣa buddhyā aham staumi tvām prakṛteḥ param tathā ca ajñāna bhāvena saṃstutaḥ asi mayā prabho kṛta aparādhe api hare kṣamām kurvanti sādhavaḥ tasmāt prasīda deveśa bhakta sneham samāśritaḥ yaḥ saṃpūjya jagannātham pratyaham stauti mānavaḥ trisaṃdhyam yaḥ japet vidvān idam stotra varam śuciḥ sa lokam śāśvatam viṣṇoḥ yāti nirdhūta kalmaṣaḥ na nāstikāya mūrkhāya na kṛtaghnāya mānine dātavyam bhakti yuktāya guṇa śīla anvitāya ca idam samasta agha vināśa hetuḥ kāruṇya saṃjñam sukha mokṣa dam ca aśeṣa vāñchā phala dam variṣṭham ye tam su sūkṣmam vimalāḥ murārim te mukti bhājaḥ praviśanti viṣṇum paraḥ pareṣām na tatas asti ca anyat draṣṭā sa pātā sa tu nāśa kartā kim vidyayā kim sva guṇaiḥ ca teṣām yajñaiḥ ca dānaiḥ ca tapobhiḥ ugraiḥ yeṣām na bhaktiḥ bhavati iha kṛṣṇe makhaiḥ tapobhiḥ sa guṇaiḥ variṣṭhaḥ jñātā sa dātā sa tu satya vaktā stutvā evam muni śārdūlāḥ praṇamya ca sanātanam cintā āviṣṭaḥ mahīpālaḥ kuśān āstīrya bhū tale vastram ca tad manāḥ bhūtvā suṣvāpa dharaṇī tale śārṅga bāṇa dharam devam jvalat tejoti maṇḍalam yuga anta āditya varṇa ābham nīla vaidūrya saṃnibham suparṇa aṃse tam āsīnam ṣoḍaśa ardha bhujam śubham sa ca asmai prābravīt dhīrāḥ sādhu rājan mahāmate tuṣṭaḥ asmi te mahīpāla vṛthā kim anuśocasi yat atra pratimā rājan jagat pūjyā sanātanī yathā sā prāpyate bhūpa tat upāyam bravīmi te gatāyām adya śarvaryām nirmale bhāskara udite sāgarasya jalasya ante nānā druma vibhūṣite jalam tathā eva velāyām dṛśyate tatra vai mahat kūla ante hi mahā vṛkṣaḥ sthitaḥ sthala jaleṣu ca ekākī viharan rājan sa tvam paśyasi pādapam īdṛś cihnam samālokya chedaya tvam aśaṅkitaḥ chedyamānam tu tam vṛkṣam prātar adbhuta darśanam dṛṣṭvā tena eva saṃcintya tatas bhūpāla darśanāt kuru tām pratimām divyām jahi cintām vimohinīm tām niśām sa samudvīkṣya sthitaḥ tad gata mānasaḥ vyāharan vaiṣṇavān mantrān sūktam ca eva tad ātmakam pragatāyām rajanyām tu utthitaḥ na anya mānasaḥ sa snātvā sāgare samyak yathāvat vidhinā tatas dattvā dānam ca viprebhyaḥ grāmān ca nagarāṇi ca na ca aśvaḥ na padātiḥ ca na gajaḥ na ca sārathiḥ ekākī sa mahā velām praviveśa mahīpatiḥ mahā utsedham mahā kāyam prasuptam ca jala antike sāndra māñjiṣṭha varṇa ābham nāma jāti vivarjitam naranāthaḥ tadā viprāḥ drumam dṛṣṭvā mudā anvitaḥ paraśunā śātayām āsa niśitena dṛḍhena ca nirīkṣyamāṇe kāṣṭhe tu babhūva adbhuta darśanam viśvakarmā ca viṣṇuḥ ca vipra rūpa dharau ubhau tau ūcatuḥ mahā rāja kim atra tvam kariṣyasi kimartham ca mahā bāho śātitaḥ ca vanaspatiḥ mahā sindhu taṭe ca eva katham vai śātitaḥ drumaḥ tayoḥ śrutvā vacaḥ viprāḥ sa tu rājā mudā anvitaḥ babhāṣe vacanam tābhyām mṛdulam madhuram tathā dṛṣṭvā tau brāhmaṇau tatra candra sūryau iva āgatau rājā uvāca ārādhayitum pratimām karomi iti matiḥ mama svapna ante ca samuddiṣṭaḥ bhavadbhyām śrāvitam mayā viṣṇuḥ uvāca nānā vyādhi śata āvarte jala budbuda saṃnibhe yatas te matiḥ utpannā viṣṇoḥ ārādhanāya vai dhanyaḥ tvam nṛpa śārdūla guṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ alaṃkṛtaḥ sa pura grāma nagarā catur varṇaiḥ alaṃkṛtā yatra tvam nṛpa śārdūla prajāḥ pālayitā prabhuḥ āvābhyām saha tiṣṭha tvam kathābhiḥ dharma saṃśritaḥ ayam mama sahāyaḥ tu āgataḥ śilpinām varaḥ viśvakarma samaḥ sākṣāt nipuṇaḥ sarva karmasu mayā uddiṣṭām tu pratimām karoti eṣa taṭam tyaja śrutvā evam vacanam tasya tadā rājā dvijanmanaḥ sāgarasya taṭam tyaktvā gatvā tasya samīpatas tasthau sa nṛpati śreṣṭhaḥ vṛkṣa chāye suśītale tatas tasmai sa viśvātmā dadau ājñām dvija ākṛtiḥ śilpi mukhyāya vipra indrāḥ kuruṣva pratimāḥ iti gaura aṅgam kṣīra varṇa ābham dvitīyam svastika aṅkitam lāṅgala astra dharam devam ananta ākhyam mahā balam na vijñātaḥ hi tasya antaḥ tena anantaḥ iti smṛtaḥ bhaginīm vāsudevasya rukma varṇām su śobhanām tad kṣaṇāt kārayām āsa pratimāḥ śubha lakṣaṇāḥ prathamam śukla varṇa ābham śārada indu sama prabham ārakta akṣam mahā kāyam sphaṭa a vikaṭa mastakam kuṇḍala eka dharam divyam gadā muśala dhāriṇam dvitīyam puṇḍarīkākṣam nīla jīmūta saṃnibham pīta vāsasam ati ugram śubham śrīvatsa lakṣaṇam vicitra vastra saṃchannām hāra keyūra bhūṣitām vicitra ābharaṇa upetām ratna hāra avalambitām pīna unnata kucām ramyām viśvakarmā vinirmame sa tu rāja adbhutam dṛṣṭvā kṣaṇena ekena nirmitāḥ divya vastra yuga channāḥ nānā ratnaiḥ alaṃkṛtāḥ ubhau ca adbhuta karmāṇau deva vṛttau amānuṣau devau vā mānuṣau vā api yakṣa vidyādharau yuvām kim nu brahma hṛṣīkeśau kim vasū kim uta aśvinau yuvām gataḥ asmi śaraṇam ātmā tu me prakāśyatām śrī bhagavān uvāca na aham devaḥ na yakṣaḥ vā na daityaḥ na ca devarāj ārādhanīyaḥ bhūtānām antaḥ yasya na vidyate paṭhyate sarva śāstreṣu vedānteṣu nigadyate aham eva svayam brahmā aham viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ api aham varuṇaḥ apām patiḥ ca aham dharitrī ca mahīdharaḥ carācaram ca yat viśvam mad anyat na asti kiṃcana prītaḥ aham te nṛpa śreṣṭha varam varaya suvrata mad darśanam apuṇyānām svapna ante api na jāyate tvam punar dṛḍha bhakti tvāt pratyakṣam dṛṣṭavān asi romāñcita tanuḥ bhūtvā idam stotram jagau nṛpaḥ rājā uvāca śriyaḥ kānta namaḥ te astu śrīpate pīta vāsase śabda atītam guṇa atītam bhāva abhāva vivarjitam nirlepam nirguṇam sūkṣmam sarvajñam sarva bhāvanam prāvṛṣ megha pratīkāśam go brāhmaṇa hite ratam sarveṣām eva goptāram vyāpinam sarva bhāvinam nāga paryaṅka śayanam kṣīroda arṇava śāyinam rājā uvāca parivrāj yoga yuktāḥ ca veda tattva artha cintakāḥ nirguṇam nirmalam śāntam yat paśyanti manīṣinaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca avicchinnam mahā rājyam kuru tvam nṛpa sattama cintā śoka vinirmuktam kriyā kāraṇa varjitam tat aham darśayiṣyāmi jñeya ākhyam paramam padam yam prāpya parama ānandam prāpsyasi paramām gatim yāvat samudrāḥ sapta eva yāvat meru ādi parvatāḥ tiṣṭhanti divi devāḥ ca tāvat sarvatra ca avyayā indradyumnasaraḥ nāma tīrtham yajña aṅga saṃbhavam dāpayiṣyati yaḥ piṇḍān taṭe asmin sarasaḥ śubhe sarasaḥ dakṣiṇe bhāge nairṛtyām tu samāśrite ketakī vana saṃchannaḥ nānā pādapa saṃkulaḥ pāṭalā āmrāta saralaiḥ candanaiḥ devadārubhiḥ nyagrodha aśvattha khadiraiḥ pārijātaiḥ saha arjunaiḥ hintālaiḥ ca eva tālaiḥ ca śiṃśapaiḥ badaraiḥ tathā ṛkṣe neṣyanti naḥ tatra nītvā sapta dināni vai maṇḍape sthāpayiṣyanti su veśyābhiḥ su śobhanaiḥ krīḍā viśeṣa bahulaiḥ nṛtya gīta manoharaiḥ cāmaraiḥ svarṇa daṇḍaiḥ ca vyajanaiḥ ratna bhūṣaṇaiḥ vījayantaḥ tathā asmabhyam sthāpayiṣyanti maṅgalāḥ brahmacārī yatiḥ ca eva snātakāḥ ca dvijottamāḥ vānaprasthāḥ gṛhasthāḥ ca siddhāḥ ca anye ca brāhmaṇāḥ kariṣyanti stutim rājan rāma keśavayoḥ punar naraḥ varṣa ayutam divyam śrīmat haripure vaset koṭīdhanapatiḥ śrīmān caturvedī bhavet dhruvam jagāma adarśanam viprāḥ sahitaḥ viśvakarmaṇā sa tu rājā tadā hṛṣṭaḥ romāñcita tanūruhaḥ tatas kṛṣṇam ca rāmam ca subhadrām ca vara pradām rathaiḥ vimāna saṃkāśaiḥ maṇi kāñcana citritaiḥ saṃvāhya tāḥ tadā rājā mahā maṅgala niḥsvanaiḥ nānā vāditra nirghoṣaiḥ nānā veda svanaiḥ śubhaiḥ tatas śubha tithau kāle nakṣatre śubha lakṣaṇe pratiṣṭhām kārayām āsa su muhūrte dvijaiḥ saha ācārya anumatena eva sarvam kṛtvā mahīpatiḥ ācāryāya tadā dattvā dakṣiṇām vidhivat prabhuḥ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhām vidhivat prāsāde bhavana uttame dadau ca anyān sa viṣayān purāṇi nagarāṇi ca evam bahuvidham dattvā rājyam kṛtvā yathocitam evam mayā muni śreṣṭhāḥ kathitaḥ vaḥ nṛpa uttamaḥ viṣṇuḥ uvāca āścaryam menire viprāḥ papracchuḥ ca punar mudā munayaḥ ūcuḥ devatā prekṣaṇe ca eva brūhi sarvam pṛthak pṛthak dṛṣṭvā sadā jyeṣṭha śukla dvādaśyām puruṣottamam tasmāt jyeṣṭhe muni śreṣṭhāḥ prayatnena su saṃyataiḥ pañcatīrtham tu vidhivat kṛtvā jyeṣṭhe narottamaḥ śukla pakṣasya dvādaśyām paśyet tam puruṣottamam kṛtvā tasmin pañcatīrtham draṣṭavyaḥ puruṣottamaḥ ahani ahani śuddha ātmā saḥ api viṣṇu puram vrajet yātrām karoti kṛṣṇasya śraddhayā yaḥ samāhitaḥ cakram dṛṣṭvā hareḥ dūrāt prāsāda upari saṃsthitam āsīt kalpe muni śreṣṭhāḥ saṃpravṛtte mahā kṣaye tatas saṃvartakaḥ vahniḥ vāyunā saha bho dvijāḥ nirdahati āśu gaḥ vāyuḥ sa ca saṃvartakaḥ analaḥ pradīptaḥ asau mahā raudraḥ kalpa agniḥ iti saṃśrutaḥ mahā jvālaḥ mahā arciṣmān saṃpradīpta mahā svanaḥ ekaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate viprāḥ mārkaṇḍeya iti viśrutaḥ moha pāśaiḥ nibaddhaḥ asau kṣudh tṛṣṇā ākulita indriyāḥ sa dṛṣṭvā tam mahā vahnim śuṣka kaṇṭha oṣṭha tālukaḥ tṛṣṇā ārtaḥ praskhalan viprāḥ tadā asau bhaya vihvalaḥ babhrāma pṛthivīm sarvām kāṃdiśīkaḥ vicetanaḥ na lebhe ca tadā śarma yatra viśrāmyatā dvijāḥ karomi kim na jānāmi yasya aham śaraṇam vraje katham paśyāmi tam devam puruṣa īśam sanātanam prāptavān tat padam divyam mahāpralaya kāraṇam puruṣeśam iti khyātam vaṭa rājam sanātanam āsādya tam muni śreṣṭhāḥ tasya mūle samāviśat na kālāgni bhayam tatra na ca aṅgāra pravarṣaṇam kecid kamala pattra ābhāḥ kecid hiṅgula saṃnibhāḥ āpūritāḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ salila ogha pariplutāḥ tatas tam mārutam ghoram sa viṣṇuḥ muni sattamāḥ tatas muniḥ sa viśrāntaḥ dhyātvā ca puruṣottamam dadarśa cakṣuḥ unmīlya jala pūrṇām vasuṃdharām na apaśyat tam vaṭam na urvīm na diś ādi na bhāskaram babhrāma asau muniḥ ca ārtaḥ itas ca itas ca saṃplavan nimamajja tadā viprāḥ trātāram na adhigacchati evam tam vihvalam dṛṣṭvā kṛpayā puruṣottamaḥ provāca muni śārdūlāḥ tadā dhyānena toṣitaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca āgaccha āgaccha śīghram tvam mārkaṇḍeya mama antikam uvāca sa tadā viprāḥ vismitaḥ ca abhavat muhur mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca evam uktvā tadā viprāḥ cintā āviṣṭaḥ abhavat muniḥ kim svapnaḥ ayam mayā dṛṣṭaḥ kim vā mohaḥ ayam āgataḥ sa gatvā śaraṇam devam muniḥ tad gata mānasaḥ dadarśa tam vaṭam bhūyas viśālam salila upari padmarāga ādibhiḥ juṣṭam ratnaiḥ anyaiḥ alaṃkṛtam tasya upari sthitam devam kṛṣṇam bāla vapuḥ dharam catur bhujam sundara aṅgam padma pattra āyata īkṣaṇam vanamālā vṛta uraskam divya kuṇḍala dhāriṇam mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca vṛthā me tapasaḥ vīryam vṛthā jñānam vṛthā kriyā vṛthā me jīvitam dīrgham vṛthā mānuṣyam eva ca yaḥ aham suptam na jānāmi paryaṅke divya bālakam trāṇa artham vihvalaḥ ca asau nirvedam gatavān tadā tatas bāla arka saṃkāśam sva mahimnā vyavasthitam sarva tejaḥ mayam viprāḥ na śaśāka abhivīkṣitum dṛṣṭvā tam munim āyāntam sa bālaḥ prahasan iva provāca muni śārdūlāḥ tadā megha ogha nisvanaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca vatsa jānāmi śrāntam tvām trāṇa artham mām upasthitam śarīram viśa me kṣipram viśrāmaḥ te mayā uditaḥ viveśa vadanam tasya vivṛtam ca avaśaḥ muniḥ dadarśa pṛthivīm kṛtsnām nānā janapadaiḥ vṛtām dadarśa tān samudrān ca jambu plakṣam ca śālmalam merum ca sarva ratna āḍhyam apaśyat kanakācalam nānā ratna anvitaiḥ śṛṅgaiḥ bhūṣitam bahu kandaram muni yakṣa apsarobhiḥ ca vṛtaiḥ ca anyaiḥ surālayaiḥ evam sumerum śrīmantam apaśyat muni sattamaḥ śvetam ca durdharam nīlam kailāsam mandaram girim mahendram malayam vindhyam pāriyātram tathā arbudam tatas tān tu muni śreṣṭhāḥ saḥ apaśyat ratna bhūṣitān kurukṣetram ca pāñcālān matsyān madrān sa kekayān vāhlīkān śūrasenān ca kāśmīrān taṅgaṇān khasān pārvatīyān kirātān ca karṇaprāvaraṇān marūn antyajān antya jātīn ca saḥ apaśyat tasya ca udare mṛgān śākhāmṛgān siṃhān varāhān sṛmarān śaśān gajān ca anyān tathā sattvān saḥ apaśyat tasya ca udare śakra ādīn vibudhān śreṣṭhān tathā anyān ca divaukasaḥ yat kiṃcid tena loke asmin dṛṣṭa pūrvam carācaram athavā kim bahu uktena kīrtitena punar punar mahā talam ca brahmāṇḍam apaśyat tasya ca udare avyāhatā gatiḥ tasya tadā abhūt dvijasattamāḥ bhramamāṇaḥ tadā kukṣau kṛtsnam jagat idam dvijāḥ na antam jagāma dehasya tasya viṣṇoḥ kadācana yadā asau na āgataḥ ca antam tasya dehasya bho dvijāḥ tadā tam vara dam devam śaraṇam gatavān muniḥ mahātmanaḥ mukhāt tasya vivṛtāt puruṣasya saḥ punar ca eka arṇavām urvīm apaśyat jana varjitām pūrva dṛṣṭam ca tam devam dadarśa śiśu rūpiṇam śākhāyām vaṭa vṛkṣasya paryaṅka upari saṃsthitam jagat ādāya tiṣṭhantam padma pattra āyata īkṣaṇam dṛṣṭvā mukhāt viniṣkrāntam provāca prahasan iva śrī bhagavān uvāca bhaktaḥ asi me muni śreṣṭha śrāntaḥ asi ca mama āśritaḥ tena tvām upakārāya saṃbhāṣe paśya mām iha dadarśa tam su duṣprekṣam ratnaiḥ divyaiḥ alaṃkṛtam prasannā nirmalā dṛṣṭiḥ muhūrtāt tasya bho dvijāḥ prasādāt tasya devasya prādurbhūtā punar navā kṛtāñjaliḥ tadā hṛṣṭaḥ vismitaḥ ca punar punar mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca trāhi mām cāru padma akṣa duḥkhitam śaraṇa āgatam saṃtaptaḥ asmi suraśreṣṭha saṃvarta ākhyena vahninā aṅgāra varṣa bhītam ca trāhi mām puruṣottama śoṣitaḥ ca pracaṇḍena vāyunā jagadāyunā vihvalaḥ aham tathā śrāntaḥ trāhi mām puruṣottama tāpitaḥ ca taśa amātyaiḥ pralaya āvartaka ādibhiḥ na śāntim adhigacchāmi trāhi mām puruṣottama tṛṣitaḥ ca kṣudhā āviṣṭaḥ duḥkhitaḥ ca jagatpate trātāram na atra paśyāmi trāhi mām puruṣottama na ca antam adhigacchāmi trāhi mām puruṣottama tava udare ca deveśa mayā dṛṣṭam carācaram vismitaḥ aham viṣaṇṇaḥ ca trāhi mām puruṣottama prasīda vibudhām nātha prasīda vibudha ālaya prasīda sarva loka īśa jagat kāraṇa kāraṇa prasīda kamalā kānta prasīda tridaśeśvara prasīda kaṃsa keśī ghna prasīda ariṣṭa nāśana prasīda kṛṣṇa daitya ghna prasīda danuja antaka tvam mahī tvam jalam deva tvam agniḥ tvam samīraṇaḥ puruṣaḥ tvam jagat vyāpī puruṣāt api ca uttamaḥ tvam indriyāṇi sarvāṇi śabda ādyāḥ viṣayāḥ prabho tvam dikpālāḥ ca dharmāḥ ca vedāḥ yajñāḥ sa dakṣiṇāḥ tvam yamaḥ pitṛ rāj deva tvam rakṣaḥ adhipatiḥ svayam varuṇaḥ tvam apām nātha tvam vāyuḥ tvam dhana īśvaraḥ tvam īśānaḥ tvam anantaḥ tvam gaṇeśaḥ ca ṣaṇmukhaḥ vasavaḥ tvam tathā rudrāḥ tvam ādityāḥ ca khecarāḥ dānavāḥ tvam tathā yakṣāḥ tvam daityāḥ sa marut gaṇāḥ siddhāḥ ca apsarasaḥ nāgāḥ gandharvāḥ tvam sa cāraṇāḥ pitaraḥ vālakhilyāḥ ca prajānām patayaḥ acyuta munayaḥ tvam ṛṣi gaṇāḥ tvam aśvinau niśācarāḥ anyāḥ ca jātayaḥ tvam hi yat kiṃcid jīva saṃjñitam tvam ākāśaḥ paraḥ śāntaḥ ajaḥ tvam vibhuḥ avyayaḥ evam tvām nirguṇam stotum kaḥ śaknoti nirañjanam śrī bhagavān uvāca śrutvā sa vacanam viprāḥ śiśoḥ tasya mahātmanaḥ mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca jñātum icchāmi deveśa brūhi sarvam aśeṣatas mahat etat acintyam ca yat aham dṛṣṭavān prabho śrī bhagavān uvāca tena nārāyaṇaḥ asmi uktaḥ mama tāḥ tu ayanam sadā dyauḥ mūrdhā kham diśaḥ śrotre tathā apaḥ sveda saṃbhavāḥ prāptum śakyaḥ na ca eva aham naraiḥ duṣkṛta karmabhiḥ sarva loke ca mām bhaktāḥ pūjayanti ca sarvaśas vihitaḥ sarvaḥ eva asau mayā ātmā bhūta bhāvanaḥ yāvat yugānām viprarṣe sahasram parivartate evam uktvā tadā viprāḥ punar tam prāha mādhavaḥ śrī bhagavān uvāca mune brūhi yad artham mām stutavān paramārthataḥ varam vṛṇīṣva yat śreṣṭham dadāmi nacirāt aham āyuṣmān asi devānām mad bhaktaḥ asi dṛḍha vrataḥ śrutvā vāṇīm śubhām tasya vilokya sa tadā punar mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca dṛṣṭam param hi deveśa tava rūpam dvijottama mohaḥ ayam vigataḥ satyam tvayi dṛṣṭe tu me hare śaiva bhāgavatānām ca vāda artha pratiṣedhakam tatas jñāsyanti loke asmin eka mūrtī hari īśvarau śrī bhagavān uvāca yat etat paramam devam kāraṇam bhuvana īśvaram liṅgam ārādhana arthāya nānā bhāva praśāntaye mama ādiṣṭena vipra indra kuru śīghram śiva ālayam tad prabhāvāt śiva loke tiṣṭha tvam ca tathā akṣayam na āvayoḥ antaram kiṃcid eka bhāvau dvidhā kṛtau yaḥ rudraḥ sa svayam viṣṇuḥ yaḥ viṣṇuḥ sa maheśvaraḥ tava nāma aṅkitam tasmāt kuru vipra śiva ālayam mārkaṇḍeyam muni śreṣṭhāḥ sarvavyāpī janārdanaḥ yat phalam snāna dānena devatā prekṣaṇena ca mārkaṇḍeya hradam gatvā naraḥ ca udak mukhaḥ śuciḥ nābhi mātre jale snātvā vidhivat devatāḥ ṛṣīn tila udakena matimān pitṝn ca anyān ca tarpayet mūlamantreṇa saṃpūjya mārkaṇḍeyasya ca īśvaram trāhi mām tvam virūpākṣa mahādeva namaḥ astu te mārkaṇḍeya hrade tu evam snātvā dṛṣṭvā ca śaṃkaram kalpavṛkṣam tatas gatvā kṛtvā tam tris pradakṣiṇam om namaḥ vyakta rūpāya mahāpralaya kāriṇe amaraḥ tvam sadā kalpe hareḥ ca āyatanam vaṭa bhaktyā pradakṣiṇam kṛtvā natvā kalpavaṭam naraḥ sahasā mucyate pāpāt jīrṇa tvacaḥ iva uragaḥ chāyām tasya samākramya kalpavṛkṣasya bho dvijāḥ dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇa aṅga saṃbhūtam brahma tejaḥ mayam param nyagrodha ākṛtikam viṣṇum praṇipatya ca bho dvijāḥ dṛṣṭvā vaṭam vainateyam yaḥ paśyet puruṣottamam praviśya āyatanam viṣṇoḥ kṛtvā tam tris pradakṣiṇam saṃkarṣaṇam sva mantreṇa bhaktyā āpūjya prasādayet namaḥ te balinām śreṣṭha namaḥ te dharaṇīdhara pralambāre namaḥ te astu trāhi mām kṛṣṇa pūrvaja evam prasādya ca anantam ajeyam tridaśa arcitam mahābalam haladharam kuṇḍala eka vibhūṣitam jñānam tatra samāsādya muktim prāpnoti durlabhām evam abhyarcya halinam tatas kṛṣṇam vicakṣaṇaḥ pūjayanti sadā dhīrāḥ te mokṣam prāpnuvanti vai na tām gatim surāḥ yānti yoginaḥ nā eva soma pāḥ yām gatim yānti bho viprāḥ dvādaśa akṣara tatparāḥ tasmāt tena eva mantreṇa bhaktyā kṛṣṇam jagadgurum jaya kṛṣṇa jagannātha jaya sarva agha nāśana jaya cāṇūra keśi ghna jaya kaṃsa niṣūdana saṃsāra sāgare ghore niḥsāre duḥkha phenile nimagnaḥ aham suraśreṣṭha trāhi mām puruṣottama pīna aṃsam dvi bhujam kṛṣṇam padma pattra āyata īkṣaṇam mahā uraskam mahā bāhum pīta vastram śubha ānanam naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca naraḥ tat phalam āpnoti dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam praṇamya ca dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam naraḥ bhaktyā mokṣam prāpnoti durlabham pāpaiḥ vimuktaḥ śuddha ātmā kalpa koṭi samudbhavaiḥ gandharva apsarasaiḥ sārdham yathā viṣṇuḥ catur bhujaḥ cyutaḥ tasmāt iha āyātaḥ viprāṇām pravare kule svadharma nirataḥ śāntaḥ dātā bhūta hite rataḥ āsādya vaiṣṇavam jñānam tatas muktim avāpnuyāt tatas saṃpūjya mantreṇa subhadrām bhakta vatsalām trāhi mām padma pattra akṣi kātyāyani namaḥ astu te evam prasādya tām devīm jagaddhātrīm jagat hitām baladevasya bhaginīm subhadrām vara dām śivām iha mānuṣa tām prāptaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ veda vid bhavet evam dṛṣṭvā balam kṛṣṇam subhadrām praṇipatya ca
